```
1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY,
     7→    detail_dict,
     8→    is_review_finding,
     9→    is_subjective_finding,
    10→    primary_command_for_finding,
    11→    review_finding_weight,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→    slugify,
    14→    status_matches,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import subjective_strict_scores
    18→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    19→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    20→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    21→
    22→# Plan-aware sort tiers (item_sort_key)
    23→_TIER_PLANNED = 0   # Items with explicit plan position
    24→_TIER_EXISTING = 1  # Known items, natural ranking
    25→_TIER_NEW = 2       # Newly discovered items
    26→
    27→# Natural ranking groups (_natural_sort_key)
    28→_RANK_INITIAL_REVIEW = -3  # Unassessed subjective dimensions
    29→_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE = -2    # Epic triage workflow stages
    30→_RANK_WORKFLOW = -1         # Score checkpoints, create-plan
    31→_RANK_CLUSTER = 0           # Auto-clustered findings
    32→_RANK_FINDING = 1           # Individual findings + assessed subjective
    33→
    34→
    35→def enrich_with_impact(items: list[dict], dimension_scores: dict) -> None:
    36→    """Stamp ``estimated_impact`` on each item based on dimension-level headroom.
    37→
    38→    Impact = ``overall_per_point * headroom`` where headroom = ``100 - score``.
    39→    Items in dimensions with more score headroom sort first.
    40→    """
    41→    if not dimension_scores:
    42→        for item in items:
    43→            item["estimated_impact"] = 0.0
    44→        return
    45→
    46→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_breakdown
    47→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import get_dimension_for_detector
    48→
    49→    breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores)
    50→    entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
    51→
    52→    # Build lookup: normalized dimension name -> {per_point, headroom}
    53→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    54→    for entry in entries:
    55→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    56→        if not name:
    57→            continue
    58→        per_point = float(entry.get("overall_per_point", 0.0))
    59→        score = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
    60→        headroom = 100.0 - score
    61→        dim_impact[name.lower()] = {"per_point": per_point, "headroom": headroom}
    62→
    63→    for item in items:
    64→        impact = _compute_item_impact(item, dim_impact, get_dimension_for_detector)
    65→        item["estimated_impact"] = impact
    66→
    67→
    68→def _compute_item_impact(
    69→    item: dict,
    70→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]],
    71→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    72→) -> float:
    73→    """Compute impact value for a single queue item."""
    74→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    75→
    76→    # Subjective items (synthetic dimensions or subjective findings):
    77→    # look up by detail.dimension_name
    78→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    79→        dim_name = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    80→        entry = dim_impact.get(dim_name.lower())
    81→        if entry:
    82→            return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    83→        return 0.0
    84→
    85→    # Mechanical findings: use detector -> dimension mapping
    86→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    87→    if detector:
    88→        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    89→        if dimension:
    90→            entry = dim_impact.get(dimension.name.lower())
    91→            if entry:
    92→                return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    93→
    94→    return 0.0
    95→
    96→
    97→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    98→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    99→        detail = detail_dict(item)
   100→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
   101→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
   102→
   103→
   104→def build_finding_items(
   105→    state: dict,
   106→    *,
   107→    scan_path: str | None,
   108→    status_filter: str,
   109→    scope: str | None,
   110→    chronic: bool,
   111→) -> list[dict]:
   112→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
   113→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
   114→    out: list[dict] = []
   115→
   116→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
   117→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   118→            continue
   119→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
   120→            continue
   121→        if chronic and not (
   122→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
   123→        ):
   124→            continue
   125→
   126→        # Evidence-only: skip findings below standalone confidence threshold
   127→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   128→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   129→        if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
   130→            threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
   131→            finding_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
   132→            if finding_rank > threshold_rank:
   133→                continue
   134→
   135→        item = dict(finding)
   136→        item["id"] = finding_id
   137→        item["kind"] = "finding"
   138→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
   139→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
   140→        item["review_weight"] = (
   141→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
   142→        )
   143→        subjective_score = None
   144→        if item["is_subjective"]:
   145→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
   146→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
   147→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
   148→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
   149→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
   150→            )
   151→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
   152→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
   153→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
   154→            item,
   155→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
   156→        )
   157→
   158→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
   159→            continue
   160→        out.append(item)
   161→
   162→    return out
   163→
   164→
   165→def _natural_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
   166→    """Compute natural (non-plan-aware) ranking for queue items."""
   167→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   168→
   169→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority
   170→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
   171→        return (_RANK_INITIAL_REVIEW, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))
   172→
   173→    # Triage stage items: stage order, blocked after unblocked
   174→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   175→        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
   176→        stage_index = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
   177→        return (_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
   178→
   179→    # Workflow action items (e.g. create-plan)
   180→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   181→        return (_RANK_WORKFLOW, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
   182→
   183→    if kind == "cluster":
   184→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
   185→        action_pri = ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY.get(
   186→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   187→        )
   188→        return (
   189→            _RANK_CLUSTER,
   190→            action_pri,
   191→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   192→            item.get("id", ""),
   193→        )
   194→
   195→    impact = item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0)
   196→
   197→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   198→        return (
   199→            _RANK_FINDING,
   200→            -impact,
   201→            subjective_score_value(item),
   202→            item.get("id", ""),
   203→        )
   204→
   205→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   206→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   207→    return (
   208→        _RANK_FINDING,
   209→        -impact,
   210→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   211→        -review_weight,
   212→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   213→        item.get("id", ""),
   214→    )
   215→
   216→
   217→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
   218→    """Unified sort key: plan position first, then natural ranking.
   219→
   220→    When ``_plan_position`` is stamped (by :func:`stamp_plan_sort_keys`),
   221→    planned items sort first in plan order, then existing items by natural
   222→    ranking, then newly-discovered items by natural ranking.
   223→
   224→    When no plan fields are stamped, falls back to pure natural ranking.
   225→    """
   226→    plan_pos = item.get("_plan_position")
   227→
   228→    if plan_pos is not None:
   229→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   230→        # Triage stages: even when explicitly planned, maintain the
   231→        # blocked-before-unblocked invariant for correctness.
   232→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
   233→            blocked = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
   234→            stage_idx = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
   235→            return (_TIER_PLANNED, 1, blocked, stage_idx, item.get("id", ""))
   236→        # Workflow actions (score communication, create-plan) sort before
   237→        # triage stages so the user sees score context first.
   238→        if kind == "workflow_action":
   239→            return (_TIER_PLANNED, 0, 0, plan_pos, item.get("id", ""))
   240→        return (_TIER_PLANNED, 2, 0, plan_pos, item.get("id", ""))
   241→
   242→    is_new = item.get("_is_new", False)
   243→    group = _TIER_NEW if is_new else _TIER_EXISTING
   244→    return (group, *_natural_sort_key(item))
   245→
   246→
   247→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   248→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   249→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   250→        return {
   251→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   252→            "stage": item.get("stage_name"),
   253→            "is_blocked": item.get("is_blocked", False),
   254→            "blocked_by": item.get("blocked_by", []),
   255→            "policy": "Triage stages sort by dependency order; blocked stages follow unblocked.",
   256→            "ranking_factors": ["blocked_penalty asc", "stage_index asc"],
   257→        }
   258→
   259→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   260→        return {
   261→            "kind": "workflow_action",
   262→            "policy": "Workflow items sort before triage stages and findings.",
   263→            "ranking_factors": ["id asc"],
   264→        }
   265→
   266→    if kind == "cluster":
   267→        return {
   268→            "kind": "cluster",
   269→            "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   270→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
   271→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", 0),
   272→            "policy": "Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type then size.",
   273→            "ranking_factors": ["action_type asc", "member_count desc", "id asc"],
   274→        }
   275→
   276→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   277→        initial = item.get("initial_review", False)
   278→        return {
   279→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   280→            "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   281→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   282→            "initial_review": initial,
   283→            "policy": (
   284→                "Initial review items sort first (onboarding priority)."
   285→                if initial else
   286→                "Sorted by dimension impact (score headroom × weight), then subjective score."
   287→            ),
   288→            "ranking_factors": ["estimated_impact desc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   289→        }
   290→
   291→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   292→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   293→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   294→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   295→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   296→    if is_subjective:
   297→        ranking_factors = ["estimated_impact desc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   298→    elif is_review:
   299→        ranking_factors = [
   300→            "estimated_impact desc",
   301→            "confidence asc",
   302→            "review_weight desc",
   303→            "count desc",
   304→            "id asc",
   305→        ]
   306→    else:
   307→        ranking_factors = ["estimated_impact desc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   308→    explain = {
   309→        "kind": "finding",
   310→        "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   311→        "confidence": confidence,
   312→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   313→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   314→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   315→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   316→    }
   317→    if is_review:
   318→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   319→    if is_subjective:
   320→        explain["policy"] = (
   321→            "Sorted by dimension impact (score headroom × weight), then subjective score."
   322→        )
   323→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   324→    return explain
   325→
   326→
   327→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   328→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   329→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   330→    for item in items:
   331→        if group == "file":
   332→            key = item.get("file", "")
   333→        elif group == "detector":
   334→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   335→        elif group == "cluster":
   336→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   337→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   338→        else:
   339→            key = "items"
   340→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   341→    return grouped
   342→
   343→
   344→__all__ = [
   345→    "build_finding_items",
   346→    "enrich_with_impact",
   347→    "item_explain",
   348→    "item_sort_key",
   349→    "subjective_score_value",
```

```
1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    ExecutionLogEntry,
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    empty_plan,
    10→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    13→
    14→
    15→_DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES = 10000
    16→
    17→
    18→def _get_log_cap() -> int:
    19→    """Read execution_log_max_entries from config. Returns 0 for unlimited."""
    20→    try:
    21→        from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    22→
    23→        config = load_config()
    24→        value = config.get("execution_log_max_entries", _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES)
    25→        return max(0, int(value))
    26→    except (ImportError, OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
    27→        return _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES
    28→
    29→
    30→def append_log_entry(
    31→    plan: PlanModel,
    32→    action: str,
    33→    *,
    34→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    35→    cluster_name: str | None = None,
    36→    actor: str = "user",
    37→    note: str | None = None,
    38→    detail: dict | None = None,
    39→) -> None:
    40→    """Append a structured entry to the plan's execution log."""
    41→    log = plan.get("execution_log", [])
    42→    entry: ExecutionLogEntry = {
    43→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    44→        "action": action,
    45→        "finding_ids": finding_ids or [],
    46→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
    47→        "actor": actor,
    48→        "note": note,
    49→        "detail": detail or {},
    50→    }
    51→    log.append(entry)
    52→    cap = _get_log_cap()
    53→    if cap > 0 and len(log) > cap:
    54→        plan["execution_log"] = log[-cap:]
    55→
    56→
    57→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    58→# Helpers
    59→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    60→
    61→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    62→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    63→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    64→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    65→    if finding_id in order:
    66→        order.remove(finding_id)
    67→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    68→
    69→
    70→def _resolve_position(
    71→    order: list[str],
    72→    position: str,
    73→    target: str | None = None,
    74→    offset: int | None = None,
    75→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    76→) -> int:
    77→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    78→
    79→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    80→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    81→    """
    82→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    83→
    84→    if position == "top":
    85→        return 0
    86→    if position == "bottom":
    87→        return len(order)
    88→
    89→    if position in {"before", "after"} and target:
    90→        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)
    91→
    92→    if position in {"up", "down"} and offset is not None:
    93→        return _resolve_offset_position(
    94→            order,
    95→            moving,
    96→            position=position,
    97→            offset=offset,
    98→            finding_ids=finding_ids,
    99→        )
   100→
   101→    return len(order)
   102→
   103→
   104→def _resolve_relative_position(
   105→    order: list[str],
   106→    moving: set[str],
   107→    *,
   108→    position: str,
   109→    target: str,
   110→) -> int:
   111→    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
   112→        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
   113→            return i if position == "before" else i + 1
   114→    return 0 if position == "before" else len(order)
   115→
   116→
   117→def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:
   118→    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):
   119→        if item_id == target:
   120→            return i
   121→    return None
   122→
   123→
   124→def _resolve_offset_position(
   125→    order: list[str],
   126→    moving: set[str],
   127→    *,
   128→    position: str,
   129→    offset: int,
   130→    finding_ids: list[str] | None,
   131→) -> int:
   132→    if not finding_ids:
   133→        return 0 if position == "up" else len(order)
   134→    first_id = finding_ids[0]
   135→    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
   136→    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)
   137→    if current_idx is None:
   138→        if position == "up":
   139→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
   140→        return len(clean_order)
   141→    if position == "up":
   142→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
   143→    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
   144→
   145→
   146→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   147→# Move items
   148→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   149→
   150→def move_items(
   151→    plan: PlanModel,
   152→    finding_ids: list[str],
   153→    position: str,
   154→    target: str | None = None,
   155→    offset: int | None = None,
   156→) -> int:
   157→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   158→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   159→
   160→    # Triage stage IDs are workflow-managed and cannot be manually reordered
   161→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import TRIAGE_IDS
   162→    finding_ids = [fid for fid in finding_ids if fid not in TRIAGE_IDS]
   163→    if not finding_ids:
   164→        return 0
   165→
   166→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   167→
   168→    # Remove from skipped if present
   169→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   170→    for fid in finding_ids:
   171→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   172→
   173→    # Remove from current position in order
   174→    for fid in finding_ids:
   175→        if fid in order:
   176→            order.remove(fid)
   177→
   178→    # Resolve insertion point
   179→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   180→
   181→    # Insert in original order
   182→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   183→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   184→
   185→    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.
   186→    # Moving to bottom/down/after should not create a promotion barrier.
   187→    if position == "top":
   188→        promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   189→        existing_promoted = set(promoted)
   190→        for fid in finding_ids:
   191→            if fid not in existing_promoted:
   192→                promoted.append(fid)
   193→                existing_promoted.add(fid)
   194→        plan["promoted_ids"] = promoted
   195→
   196→    return len(finding_ids)
   197→
   198→
   199→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   200→# Skip / unskip
   201→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   202→
   203→def skip_items(
   204→    plan: PlanModel,
   205→    finding_ids: list[str],
   206→    *,
   207→    kind: str = "temporary",
   208→    reason: str | None = None,
   209→    note: str | None = None,
   210→    attestation: str | None = None,
   211→    review_after: int | None = None,
   212→    scan_count: int = 0,
   213→) -> int:
   214→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   215→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   216→    now = utc_now()
   217→    count = 0
   218→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   219→    skip_set = set(finding_ids)
   220→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   221→    plan["promoted_ids"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in skip_set]
   222→    for fid in finding_ids:
   223→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   224→        skipped[fid] = {
   225→            "finding_id": fid,
   226→            "kind": kind,
   227→            "reason": reason,
   228→            "note": note,
   229→            "attestation": attestation,
   230→            "created_at": now,
   231→            "review_after": review_after,
   232→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   233→        }
   234→        count += 1
   235→    return count
   236→
   237→
   238→def unskip_items(
   239→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   240→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   241→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   242→
   243→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   244→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   245→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   246→    """
   247→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   248→    count = 0
   249→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   250→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   251→    for fid in finding_ids:
   252→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   253→        if entry is not None:
   254→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   255→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   256→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   257→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   258→            count += 1
   259→    return count, need_reopen
   260→
   261→
   262→def resurface_stale_skips(
   263→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   264→) -> list[str]:
   265→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   266→
   267→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   268→    """
   269→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   270→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   271→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   272→    for fid in list(skipped):
   273→        entry = skipped[fid]
   274→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   275→            continue
   276→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   277→        if review_after is None:
   278→            continue
   279→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   280→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   281→            skipped.pop(fid)
   282→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   283→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   284→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   285→    return resurfaced
   286→
   287→
   288→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   289→# Describe / annotate
   290→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   291→
   292→def describe_finding(
   293→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   294→) -> None:
   295→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   296→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   297→    now = utc_now()
   298→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   299→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   300→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   301→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   302→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   303→
   304→
   305→def annotate_finding(
   306→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   307→) -> None:
   308→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   309→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   310→    now = utc_now()
   311→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   312→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   313→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   314→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   315→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   316→
   317→
   318→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   319→# Reset
   320→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   321→
   322→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   323→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp.
   324→
   325→    Sets ``plan_start_scores`` to a sentinel so the next scan seeds real
   326→    scores instead of incorrectly treating the reset as a completed cycle.
   327→    """
   328→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   329→    plan.clear()
   330→    for k, v in empty_plan().items():
   331→        plan[k] = v
   332→    plan["created"] = created
   333→    # Sentinel: truthy so ``not plan.get("plan_start_scores")`` is False,
   334→    # but recognized by _seed_plan_start_scores as needing replacement.
   335→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {"reset": True}
   336→
   337→
   338→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   339→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   340→
   341→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   342→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   343→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   344→    """
   345→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   346→    found = 0
   347→
   348→    purge_set = set(finding_ids)
   349→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   350→    plan["promoted_ids"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in purge_set]
   351→
   352→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   353→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   354→    for fid in finding_ids:
   355→        was_present = False
   356→        if fid in order:
   357→            order.remove(fid)
   358→            was_present = True
   359→        if fid in skipped:
   360→            skipped.pop(fid)
   361→            was_present = True
   362→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   363→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   364→            if fid in ids:
   365→                ids.remove(fid)
   366→                was_present = True
   367→        # Clear stale cluster reference from override (preserve notes/descriptions)
   368→        override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   369→        if override and override.get("cluster"):
   370→            override["cluster"] = None
   371→            override["updated_at"] = utc_now()
   372→        if was_present:
   373→            found += 1
   374→
   375→    return found
   376→
   377→
   378→__all__ = [
   379→    "add_to_cluster",
   380→    "annotate_finding",
   381→    "append_log_entry",
   382→    "clear_focus",
   383→    "create_cluster",
   384→    "delete_cluster",
   385→    "describe_finding",
   386→    "merge_clusters",
   387→    "move_cluster",

   392→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   393→    "set_focus",

   396→]
```

```
1→"""plan command: generate prioritized markdown plan from state.
     2→
     3→This module preserves the original import path. The full plan command
     4→(with subcommands) lives in ``desloppify.app.commands.plan.cmd``.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import argparse
    10→
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_agent_plan
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    14→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import warn_best_effort
    15→from desloppify.engine import planning as planning_mod
    16→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    17→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    18→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_config_staleness
    19→
    20→
    21→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    22→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    23→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    24→    state = runtime.state
    25→
    26→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    27→        return
    28→
    29→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(runtime.config)
    30→    if config_warning:
    31→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    32→
    33→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    34→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    35→
    36→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    37→    if output:
    38→        try:
    39→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    40→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    41→            print_agent_plan(
    42→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    43→                next_command=next_command,
    44→            )
    45→        except OSError as e:
    46→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    47→    else:
    48→        print(plan_md)
    49→        print()
    50→        print_agent_plan(
    51→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
    52→            next_command=next_command,
    53→        )
    54→
    55→
    56→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_output"]
    57→
```

```
1→"""Central command registry for CLI command handler resolution."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→CommandHandler = Callable[[Any], None]
     9→
    10→_COMMAND_HANDLERS: dict[str, CommandHandler] | None = None
    11→
    12→
    13→def _build_handlers() -> dict[str, CommandHandler]:
    14→    """Import all command modules and build the handler dict on first access."""
    15→    from desloppify.app.commands.config_cmd import cmd_config
    16→    from desloppify.app.commands.detect import cmd_detect
    17→    from desloppify.app.commands.dev_cmd import cmd_dev
    18→    from desloppify.app.commands.exclude_cmd import cmd_exclude
    19→    from desloppify.app.commands.fix.cmd import cmd_fix
    20→    from desloppify.app.commands.langs import cmd_langs
    21→    from desloppify.app.commands.move.move import cmd_move
    22→    from desloppify.app.commands.next import cmd_next
    23→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cmd import cmd_plan
    24→    from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.suppress_cmd import cmd_suppress_pattern
    25→    from desloppify.app.commands.review import cmd_review
    26→    from desloppify.app.commands.scan import cmd_scan
    27→    from desloppify.app.commands.show.cmd import cmd_show
    28→    from desloppify.app.commands.status_cmd import cmd_status
    29→    from desloppify.app.commands.update_skill import cmd_update_skill
    30→    from desloppify.app.commands.viz_cmd import cmd_tree, cmd_viz
    31→    from desloppify.app.commands.zone_cmd import cmd_zone
    32→
    33→    return {
    34→        "scan": cmd_scan,
    35→        "status": cmd_status,
    36→        "show": cmd_show,
    37→        "next": cmd_next,
    38→        "suppress": cmd_suppress_pattern,
    39→        "exclude": cmd_exclude,
    40→        "autofix": cmd_fix,
    41→        "plan": cmd_plan,
    42→        "detect": cmd_detect,
    43→        "tree": cmd_tree,
    44→        "viz": cmd_viz,
    45→        "move": cmd_move,
    46→        "zone": cmd_zone,
    47→        "review": cmd_review,
    48→        "config": cmd_config,
    49→        "dev": cmd_dev,
    50→        "langs": cmd_langs,
    51→        "update-skill": cmd_update_skill,
    52→    }
    53→
    54→
    55→def get_command_handlers() -> dict[str, CommandHandler]:
    56→    """Return cached command handler dict, building on first access."""
    57→    global _COMMAND_HANDLERS
    58→    if _COMMAND_HANDLERS is None:
    59→        _COMMAND_HANDLERS = _build_handlers()
    60→    return _COMMAND_HANDLERS
    61→
    62→
    63→__all__ = ["CommandHandler", "get_command_handlers"]
    64→
```

```
_DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES = 10000


def _get_log_cap() -> int:
    """Read execution_log_max_entries from config. Returns 0 for unlimited."""
    try:
        from desloppify.core.config import load_config

        config = load_config()
        value = config.get("execution_log_max_entries", _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES)
        return max(0, int(value))
    except (ImportError, OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
        return _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES
```

```
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Focus
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
    """Set the active cluster focus."""
    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name


def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    plan["active_cluster"] = None
```

```
"""Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.

    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
    """
    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    found = 0

    purge_set = set(finding_ids)
    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
    plan["promoted_ids"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in purge_set]

    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
    for fid in finding_ids:
        was_present = False
        if fid in order:
            order.remove(fid)
            was_present = True
        if fid in skipped:
            skipped.pop(fid)
            was_present = True
        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
            if fid in ids:
                ids.remove(fid)
                was_present = True
        # Clear stale cluster reference from override (preserve notes/descriptions)
        override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
        if override and override.get("cluster"):
            override["cluster"] = None
            override["updated_at"] = utc_now()
        if was_present:
            found += 1

    return found
```

```
    18→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.queue_render import cmd_plan_queue
    19→from desloppify.app.commands.plan_cmd import cmd_plan_output as _plan_output_impl
    20→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    21→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    22→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    23→    append_log_entry,
    24→    load_plan,
    25→    purge_ids,
    26→    reset_plan,
    27→    save_plan,
    28→)
    29→
    30→
    31→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    32→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    33→    # Auto-resolve the create-plan workflow item when plan runs
    34→    plan = load_plan()
    35→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    36→        purge_ids(plan, [WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID])
    37→        save_plan(plan)
    38→    _plan_output_impl(args)
    39→
    40→
    41→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    42→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    43→    plan = load_plan()
    44→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    45→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    46→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    47→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    48→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    49→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    50→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    51→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    52→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    53→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    54→
    55→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    56→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    57→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    58→
    59→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    60→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
```

```
    27→def flatten_cli_keys(items: list[dict], *, max_items: int = 3) -> str:
    28→    """Flatten CLI keys across up to max_items subjective entries, preserving order."""
    29→    seen: set[str] = set()
    30→    ordered: list[str] = []
    31→    for item in items[:max_items]:
    32→        for key in item.get("cli_keys", []):

    35→            ordered.append(key)
    36→            seen.add(key)
    37→    return ",".join(ordered)
```

```
"""Unified queue-resolution context.

A frozen ``QueueContext`` computed once per operation replaces the scattered
``plan`` / ``target_strict`` / ``policy`` threading through function chains.
Callers build one context and pass it everywhere — makes the wrong thing
impossible.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    SubjectiveVisibility,
    compute_subjective_visibility,
)
from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel

# Sentinel: "auto-load plan from disk" (the default).
_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD = object()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QueueContext:
    """Immutable snapshot of resolved queue parameters.

    Built once via :func:`queue_context`, then threaded through
    ``build_work_queue``, ``plan_aware_queue_breakdown``, and command
    helpers so every call site agrees on plan, target, and policy.
    """

    plan: dict | None
    target_strict: float
    policy: SubjectiveVisibility


def queue_context(
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    config: dict | None = None,
    plan: dict | None | object = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    target_strict: float | None = None,
) -> QueueContext:
    """Build a :class:`QueueContext` with all parameters resolved.

    Resolution order:

    1. **plan** — explicit value wins; sentinel ``_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD`` triggers
       ``load_plan()`` (guarded by ``PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS``).
    2. **target_strict** — explicit float wins; ``None`` reads from *config*
```

## Aesthetic Warts Report - Desloppify Codebase

I've completed a thorough sweep of the desloppify codebase. Here are the genuine aesthetic warts I found:

### 1. **Stale Comments Referencing Old Module Organization**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_subjective.py`

**Lines:** 12, 121, 284

**Issue:** Comments reference module consolidation from when separate files existed:
- Line 12: `# Common helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_common)`
- Line 121: `# Integrity and dimension-mapping helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_integrity)`
- Line 284: `# Output-oriented helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_output)`

These describe old module names that no longer exist. The file was consolidated from three separate modules into one, but the comments still reference the old names. This is misleading to developers reading the code.

**Suggestion:** Update comments to reflect current organization, or remove the historical references entirely. Consider: `# Common helpers` instead of including the "formerly" reference.

---

### 2. **Inconsistent Command File Naming Convention**
**Files:** `desloppify/app/commands/` directory

**Issue:** There are two inconsistent naming patterns for command files:
- Files WITH `_cmd` suffix: `config_cmd.py`, `dev_cmd.py`, `exclude_cmd.py`, `plan_cmd.py`, `status_cmd.py`, `viz_cmd.py`, `zone_cmd.py`
- Files WITHOUT suffix: `detect.py`, `langs.py`, `next.py`
- Command subpackages have their own `cmd.py`: `plan/cmd.py`, `fix/cmd.py`, `resolve/cmd.py`, `review/cmd.py`, `scan/cmd.py`, `show/cmd.py`, `move/move.py`

This creates visual confusion about which files contain CLI command entry points. Files like `detect.py`, `langs.py`, and `next.py` don't follow the `_cmd` suffix pattern despite being top-level commands.

**Suggestion:** Standardize naming: either all should use `_cmd` suffix (e.g., `detect_cmd.py`, `langs_cmd.py`, `next_cmd.py`) OR establish a clear convention that subpackages use `cmd.py` while top-level commands use no suffix.

---

### 3. **Duplicate Confidence Order Constant Definition**
**Files:** 
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/noise.py` (line 16)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/common.py` (line 5)

**Issue:** `CONFIDENCE_ORDER` (dict mapping confidence levels to sort order) is defined in two places with identical values:
```python
# In noise.py (private, with underscore):
_CONFIDENCE_ORDER = {"high": 0, "medium": 1, "low": 2}

# In planning/common.py (public):
CONFIDENCE_ORDER = {"high": 0, "medium": 1, "low": 2}
```

The private version in `noise.py` is used only within that module for filtering findings. The public version in `planning/common.py` is the canonical one used throughout the codebase (work_queue, plan, show commands). Having two copies creates maintenance risk if one needs to be updated.

**Suggestion:** Remove the private `_CONFIDENCE_ORDER` from `noise.py` and import `CONFIDENCE_ORDER` from `desloppify.engine.planning.common` instead.

---

### 4. **Unused Import with Backward-Compatibility Comment**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/cmd.py`

**Line:** 14

**Issue:** 
```python
from desloppify.core import config as config_mod  # noqa: F401 (compat export)
```

This import is explicitly marked as unused with a suppression comment, indicating it exists only for backward compatibility. While this is legitimate, it's a code smell that could confuse maintainers.

**Suggestion:** Consider moving this to a dedicated `_compat.py` file or clearly document the compatibility contract at the module level in a docstring to make intent clearer.

---

### 5. **Backward-Compatibility Alias Without Clear Documentation**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/reporting/agent_context.py`

**Line:** 387-388

**Issue:**
```python
# Backward-compatible alias for older imports/tests.
_print_llm_summary = print_llm_summary
```

While technically correct, the comment "for older imports/tests" is vague. It doesn't explain:
- When this alias will be removed (if ever)
- What the migration path is for users of the old name
- Whether it's actually used by anything

**Suggestion:** Expand the comment to be more specific: `# Backward-compatible alias (v0.9.0+); deprecated — use print_llm_summary directly`

---

### 6. **Missing Public Exports in Operations Facade**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`

**Line:** 304-312

**Issue:** The `__all__` list includes `"collapse_clusters"`, but this function is imported from `plan_order`, not defined in `core.py`. While the re-export itself is correct, it's unclear whether this was intentional or accidental. The comment pattern suggests it should be there (since it's exported from the `work_queue.py` facade), but it makes the `__all__` list a bit confusing to read.

**Suggestion:** This is minor and technically correct, but consider adding a comment: `# re-exported from plan_order` next to the import for clarity.

---

### Summary of Warts by Severity

**High Severity (affects clarity/correctness):**
1. Stale "formerly" comments in scan_reporting_subjective.py (misleading to new readers)
2. Duplicate CONFIDENCE_ORDER constant (maintenance risk)

**Medium Severity (consistency/polish):**
3. Inconsistent command file naming convention (visual confusion)
4. Unused imports marked with noqa comments (code smell)

**Low Severity (documentation/clarity):**
5. Vague backward-compatibility comments (could be clearer)
6. Implicit re-export of collapse_clusters in __all__ (minor clarity issue)

All other areas I checked (type annotations, exception handling, function naming, __all__ completeness, dead imports) appear to be well-maintained.

Do an extremely thorough sweep of the desloppify codebase looking for the tiniest aesthetic warts. The big stuff has been fixed already — now I want the small, subtle things that make code look slightly unpolished.

Things to look for:

1. **Stale docstring references** — docstrings that reference function names, parameters, or module names that have been renamed. Check `:func:`, `:class:`, `:param:` references in docstrings.
2. **Inconsistent spacing in similar constructs** — e.g. some dicts use `{"key": val}` and nearby ones use `{ "key": val }`, or inconsistent blank lines between similar functions
3. **Orphaned backward-compat shims** — aliases, re-exports, or wrapper functions marked as backward-compatible that may no longer have callers
4. **Comments that state the obvious** — `# Return the result` before `return result`, `# Check if None` before `if x is None:`
5. **Stale TODO/FIXME/HACK/XXX comments** — especially ones that reference completed work
6. **Inconsistent __all__ patterns** — some modules alphabetize, others don't; some include re-exports, others don't
7. **Magic strings used in multiple places** that should be constants — e.g. status strings like "open", "resolved" used as literals everywhere
8. **Unnecessary parentheses or line continuations** — e.g. `return (value)` instead of `return value`
9. **Inconsistent error message formatting** — some use f-strings, some use % formatting, some capitalize, some don't
10. **Stale entries in __all__** that reference deleted or renamed symbols
11. **Imports that could be simplified** — e.g. `from foo import bar as bar` or importing a module just to access one attribute
12. **Commented-out code** that should just be deleted
13. **Trailing commas inconsistency** in similar list/dict/tuple constructs within the same file

```
    54→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    55→    persisted = config.get("exclude", [])
    56→    combined = list(cli_exclusions) + [e for e in persisted if e not in cli_exclusions]
    57→    if not combined:
    58→        return
    59→    set_exclusions(combined)
    60→    if cli_exclusions:
    61→        print(
    62→            colorize(f"  Excluding: {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"),
    63→            file=sys.stderr,
    64→        )
    65→        return
    66→    print(
    67→        colorize(
    68→            f"  Excluding (from config): {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"
    69→        ),
    70→        file=sys.stderr,
    71→    )
    72→
    73→
    74→def _resolve_default_path(args) -> None:
    75→    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.
    76→
    77→    For the review command, the last scan path (stored in state) is used as the
    78→    default so that ``desloppify review --prepare`` works on the same scope as
    79→    the preceding scan even when the project files are not under ``src/``.
    80→    """
    81→    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
    82→        return
    83→    runtime_root = (
    84→        PROJECT_ROOT.resolve()
    85→        if PROJECT_ROOT is not _PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL and isinstance(PROJECT_ROOT, Path)
    86→        else get_project_root()
    87→    )
    88→    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
    89→        try:
    90→            state_file = state_path(args)
    91→            if state_file:
    92→                saved = load_state(state_file)
    93→                saved_path = saved.get("scan_path")
    94→                if saved_path:
    95→                    args.path = str((runtime_root / saved_path).resolve())
    96→                    return
    97→        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
    98→            logger.debug("Failed to resolve default path from saved state: %s", exc)
    99→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   100→    if lang:
   101→        args.path = str(runtime_root / lang.default_src)
   102→    else:
   103→        args.path = str(get_default_path())
   104→
   105→
   106→def _load_shared_runtime(args) -> None:
   107→    """Load config/state and attach shared objects to parsed args."""
   108→    config = load_config()
   109→
   110→    state_file = state_path(args)
   111→    state = load_state(state_file)
   112→    _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config)
   113→
   114→    args.runtime = CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
   115→
   116→
   117→def _resolve_handler(command: str):
   118→    return get_command_handlers()[command]
   119→
   120→
   121→def _handle_help_command(args, parser) -> None:
   122→    """Handle explicit help command when present in parser config."""
   123→    topic = list(getattr(args, "topic", []) or [])
   124→    try:
   125→        parser.parse_args([*topic, "--help"])
   126→    except SystemExit:
   127→        return
   128→
   129→
   130→def main() -> None:
   131→    # Ensure Unicode output works on Windows terminals (cp1252 etc.)
   132→    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
   133→        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
   134→            try:
   135→                stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   136→            except (AttributeError, OSError):
   137→                logger.debug(
   138→                    "Skipping stream reconfigure for %s (not supported)",
   139→                    getattr(stream, "name", "<stream>"),
   140→                )
   141→
   142→    parser = create_parser()
   143→    args = parser.parse_args()
   144→    if args.command == "help":
   145→        _handle_help_command(args, parser)
   146→        return
   147→
   148→    try:
   149→        with runtime_scope():
   150→            _resolve_default_path(args)
   151→            _load_shared_runtime(args)
   152→
   153→            handler = _resolve_handler(args.command)
   154→            handler(args)
   155→    except CommandError as exc:
   156→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   157→        sys.exit(exc.exit_code)
   158→    except LangResolutionError as exc:
   159→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   160→        sys.exit(1)
   161→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   162→        print("\nInterrupted.")
   163→        sys.exit(1)
   164→
   165→
   166→if __name__ == "__main__":
   167→    main()
   168→
```

```
1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
     9→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    10→    compute_subjective_visibility,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import QueueContext
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    14→    ALL_STATUSES,
    15→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    16→    scope_matches,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import (
    19→    build_communicate_score_item,
    20→    build_create_plan_item,
    21→    build_import_scores_item,
    22→    build_subjective_items,
    23→    build_triage_stage_items,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    26→    build_finding_items,
    27→    enrich_with_impact,
    28→    group_queue_items,
    29→    item_explain,
    30→    item_sort_key,
    31→)
    32→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import (
    33→    collapse_clusters,
    34→    enrich_plan_metadata,
    35→    filter_cluster_focus,
    36→    new_item_ids as _new_item_ids,
    37→    separate_skipped,
    38→    stamp_plan_sort_keys,
    39→    stamp_positions,
    40→)
    41→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    42→
    43→# Sentinel: "read scan_path from state" (the safe default).
    44→# Callers that want to override can pass an explicit str or None.
    45→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE = object()
    46→
    47→
    48→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    49→class QueueBuildOptions:
    50→    """Configuration for queue construction.
    51→
    52→    ``scan_path`` defaults to reading from ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers
    53→    don't need to thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` or ``None``
    54→    to override (``None`` disables scope filtering).
    55→    """
    56→
    57→    # Output control
    58→    count: int | None = 1
    59→    explain: bool = False
    60→
    61→    # Scope filtering
    62→    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
    63→    scope: str | None = None
    64→    status: str = "open"
    65→    chronic: bool = False
    66→
    67→    # Subjective gating
    68→    include_subjective: bool = True
    69→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    70→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None
    71→
    72→    # Plan integration
    73→    plan: dict | None = None
    74→    include_skipped: bool = False
    75→    cluster: str | None = None
    76→
    77→    # Pre-computed context (overrides plan/policy)
    78→    context: QueueContext | None = None
    79→
    80→
    81→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    82→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    83→
    84→    items: list[dict]
    85→    total: int
    86→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    87→    new_ids: set[str]
    88→
    89→
    90→def build_work_queue(
    91→    state: StateModel,
    92→    *,
    93→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    94→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    95→    """Build a ranked work queue from state findings.
    96→
    97→    Pipeline:
    98→    1. Gather — finding items, subjective dimensions, workflow stages
    99→    2. Score  — estimate impact from dimension headroom, apply floor
   100→    3. Order  — stamp plan positions, sort, filter to cluster focus
   101→    4. Limit  — truncate to count, optionally add explain metadata
   102→    """
   103→    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   104→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = _resolve_inputs(opts, state)
   105→
   106→    # 1. Gather
   107→    items = build_finding_items(
   108→        state, scan_path=scan_path, status_filter=status,
   109→        scope=opts.scope, chronic=opts.chronic,
   110→    )
   111→    items += _gather_subjective_items(state, opts, plan, threshold)
   112→    items += _gather_workflow_items(state, plan, status)
   113→
   114→    # 2. Score & filter
   115→    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   116→    items = [i for i in items if _passes_impact_floor(i)]
   117→
   118→    # 3. Plan-aware ordering
   119→    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)
   120→    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   121→    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)
   122→
   123→    # 4. Finalize
   124→    if not items:
   125→        items += _empty_queue_fallback(plan)
   126→    total = len(items)
   127→    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
   128→        items = items[:opts.count]
   129→    if opts.explain:
   130→        for item in items:
   131→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   132→
   133→    return {
   134→        "items": items,
   135→        "total": total,
   136→        "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"),
   137→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   138→    }
   139→
   140→
   141→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   142→# Pipeline helpers (private to this module)
   143→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   144→
   145→
   146→def _resolve_inputs(
   147→    opts: QueueBuildOptions, state: StateModel,
   148→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None, str, float]:
   149→    """Resolve plan, scan_path, status, and subjective threshold from options."""
   150→    ctx = opts.context
   151→    plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else opts.plan
   152→
   153→    scan_path: str | None = (
   154→        state.get("scan_path")
   155→        if opts.scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
   156→        else opts.scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
   157→    )
   158→
   159→    status = opts.status
   160→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   161→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   162→
   163→    try:
   164→        threshold = float(opts.subjective_threshold)
   165→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   166→        threshold = 100.0
   167→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, threshold))
   168→
   169→    return plan, scan_path, status, threshold
   170→
   171→
   172→def _gather_subjective_items(
   173→    state: StateModel,
   174→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   175→    plan: dict | None,
   176→    threshold: float,
   177→) -> list[dict]:
   178→    """Build synthetic subjective items, gated by SubjectiveVisibility policy."""
   179→    if not opts.include_subjective:
   180→        return []
   181→    if opts.status not in {"open", "all"}:
   182→        return []
   183→    if opts.chronic:
   184→        return []
   185→
   186→    ctx = opts.context
   187→    policy = (
   188→        (ctx.policy if ctx is not None else None)
   189→        or opts.policy
   190→        or compute_subjective_visibility(state, plan=plan)
   191→    )
   192→
   193→    candidates = build_subjective_items(
   194→        state, state.get("findings", {}), threshold=threshold,
   195→    )
   196→
   197→    # When a plan explicitly includes a subjective item in queue_order,
   198→    # surface it regardless of policy — the plan is authoritative.
   199→    plan_queue_set: set[str] = (
   200→        set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   201→        if plan
   202→        else set()
   203→    )
   204→
   205→    result: list[dict] = []
   206→    for item in candidates:
   207→        if not scope_matches(item, opts.scope):
   208→            continue
   209→        item_id = item.get("id", "")
   210→        if not policy.should_surface(item) and item_id not in plan_queue_set:
   211→            continue
   212→        result.append(item)
   213→    return result
   214→
   215→
   216→def _gather_workflow_items(
   217→    state: StateModel, plan: dict | None, status: str,
   218→) -> list[dict]:
   219→    """Inject triage stages, checkpoints, and create-plan when plan is active."""
   220→    if not plan or status not in {"open", "all"}:
   221→        return []
   222→
   223→    items: list[dict] = list(build_triage_stage_items(plan, state))
   224→    for builder in (
   225→        build_import_scores_item,
   226→        build_communicate_score_item,
   227→    ):
   228→        item = builder(plan, state)
   229→        if item is not None:
   230→            items.append(item)
   231→    plan_item = build_create_plan_item(plan)
   232→    if plan_item is not None:
   233→        items.append(plan_item)
   234→    return items
   235→
   236→
   237→_MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT = 0.05
   238→
   239→
   240→def _passes_impact_floor(item: dict) -> bool:
   241→    """Return True if item should survive the impact floor filter."""
   242→    if item.get("kind") != "finding":
   243→        return True
   244→    if item.get("is_review") or item.get("is_subjective"):
   245→        return True
   246→    impact = item.get("estimated_impact")
   247→    return not impact or impact >= _MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT
   248→
   249→
   250→def _plan_presort(
   251→    items: list[dict], state: StateModel, plan: dict | None,
   252→) -> tuple[set[str], list[dict]]:
   253→    """Enrich plan metadata and stamp sort keys before sorting.
   254→
   255→    Returns ``(new_ids, skipped)`` — skipped items are removed from
   256→    ``items`` in place and returned separately for post-sort re-append.
   257→    """
   258→    if not plan:
   259→        return set(), []
   260→
   261→    new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   262→    enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)
   263→    stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids)
   264→    remaining, skipped = separate_skipped(items, plan)
   265→    items[:] = remaining
   266→    return new_ids, skipped
   267→
   268→
   269→def _plan_postsort(
   270→    items: list[dict],
   271→    skipped: list[dict],
   272→    plan: dict | None,
   273→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   274→) -> None:
   275→    """Re-append skipped items, stamp positions, filter to cluster focus."""
   276→    if not plan:
   277→        return
   278→
   279→    if opts.include_skipped:
   280→        items.extend(skipped)
   281→    stamp_positions(items, plan)
   282→    focused = filter_cluster_focus(items, plan, opts.cluster)
   283→    items[:] = focused
   284→
   285→
   286→def _empty_queue_fallback(plan: dict | None) -> list[dict]:
   287→    """Return a 'run scan' nudge when an active plan cycle has cleared."""
   288→    if not plan:
   289→        return []
   290→    plan_scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   291→    if plan_scores.get("strict") is None:
   292→        return []
   293→    return [{
   294→        "id": "workflow::run-scan",
   295→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   296→        "summary": "Queue cleared \u2014 run scan to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   297→        "primary_command": "desloppify scan",
   298→        "file": "",
   299→        "detector": "workflow",
   300→        "confidence": "high",
   301→    }]
   302→
   303→
   304→__all__ = [
   305→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   306→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   307→    "QueueContext",
   308→    "WorkQueueResult",
   309→    "build_work_queue",
   310→    "collapse_clusters",
   311→    "group_queue_items",
   312→]
   313→
```

```
1→"""Predicates and small utilities for work queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    11→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    12→)
    13→
    14→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    15→ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "refactor": 1, "manual_fix": 2, "reorganize": 3}
    16→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    17→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    18→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    19→)
    20→
    21→
    22→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    23→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    24→    detail = item.get("detail")
    25→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    26→
    27→
    28→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    29→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    30→
    31→
    32→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    33→    detector = item.get("detector")
    34→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    35→        return True
    36→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    37→        return True
    38→    return False
    39→
    40→
    41→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    42→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    """True for subjective work items, including collapsed subjective clusters."""
    47→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    48→        return True
    49→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    50→        members = item.get("members", [])
    51→        return bool(members) and all(
    52→            m.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" for m in members
    53→        )
    54→    return False
    55→
    56→
    57→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    58→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    59→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    60→    weight_by_confidence = {
    61→        "high": 1.0,
    62→        "medium": 0.7,
    63→        "low": 0.3,
    64→    }
    65→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    66→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    67→        weight *= 10.0
    68→    return float(weight)
    69→
    70→
    71→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    72→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    73→    if not scope:
    74→        return True
    75→
    76→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    77→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    78→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    79→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    80→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    81→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    82→
    83→    if "*" in scope:
    84→        return any(
    85→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    86→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    87→        )
    88→
    89→    if "::" in scope:
    90→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    91→
    92→    lowered = scope.lower()
    93→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    94→        return (
    95→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    96→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    97→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    98→        )
    99→
   100→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
   101→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
   102→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
   103→
   104→    return (
   105→        detector == scope
   106→        or filepath == scope
   107→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   108→    )
   109→
   110→
   111→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   112→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   113→
   114→
   115→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   116→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   117→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   118→    if not lang:
   119→        return None
   120→
   121→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   122→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   123→        return None
   124→
   125→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   126→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   127→        return None
   128→
   129→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   130→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   131→        return None
   132→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   133→
   134→
   135→def primary_command_for_finding(
   136→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   137→) -> str:
   138→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   139→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   140→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   141→        available_fixers = [
   142→            fixer
   143→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   144→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   145→        ]
   146→        if available_fixers:
   147→            return f"desloppify autofix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   148→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   149→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   150→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   151→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   152→    return f'desloppify plan resolve "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   153→
   154→
   155→__all__ = [
   156→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   157→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   158→    "detail_dict",
   159→    "is_review_finding",
   160→    "is_subjective_finding",
   161→    "is_subjective_queue_item",
   162→    "primary_command_for_finding",
   163→    "review_finding_weight",
   164→    "scope_matches",
   165→    "slugify",
   166→    "status_matches",
   167→    "supported_fixers_for_item",
   168→]
   169→
```
