1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.state import StateModel
     6→
     7→
     8→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
     9→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    10→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    11→    if not scan_history:
    12→        return set()
    13→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    14→    if not threshold:
    15→        return set()
    16→    return {
    17→        finding_id
    18→        for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    19→        if finding.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    20→    }
    21→
    22→
    23→def enrich_plan_metadata(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> None:
    24→    """Stamp plan description, note, and cluster info from overrides."""
    25→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    26→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    27→
    28→    for item in items:
    29→        override = overrides.get(item["id"], {})
    30→        if override.get("description"):
    31→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    32→        if override.get("note"):
    33→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    34→        if override.get("cluster"):
    35→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    36→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    37→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    38→                "name": cluster_name,
    39→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    40→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    41→            }
    42→
    43→
    44→def stamp_plan_sort_keys(
    45→    items: list[dict],
    46→    plan: dict,
    47→    new_ids: set[str],
    48→) -> None:
    49→    """Stamp ``_plan_position`` and ``_is_new`` on each item.
    50→
    51→    These fields are consumed by :func:`item_sort_key` in ``ranking.py``
    52→    to produce the correct final ordering in a single sort pass.
    53→    """
    54→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    55→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    56→
    57→    position_map: dict[str, int] = {}
    58→    for idx, finding_id in enumerate(queue_order):
    59→        if finding_id not in skipped_ids:
    60→            position_map[finding_id] = idx
    61→
    62→    for item in items:
    63→        item_id = item["id"]
    64→        pos = position_map.get(item_id)
    65→        item["_plan_position"] = pos  # None if not in queue_order
    66→        item["_is_new"] = item_id in new_ids
    67→
    68→
    69→def separate_skipped(
    70→    items: list[dict],
    71→    plan: dict,
    72→) -> tuple[list[dict], list[dict]]:
    73→    """Separate skipped items from the main list.
    74→
    75→    Returns ``(non_skipped, skipped)`` so callers can optionally re-append.
    76→    """
    77→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    78→    if not skipped_ids:
    79→        return items, []
    80→    non_skipped: list[dict] = []
    81→    skipped: list[dict] = []
    82→    for item in items:
    83→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
    84→            skipped.append(item)
    85→        else:
    86→            non_skipped.append(item)
    87→    return non_skipped, skipped
    88→
    89→
    90→def filter_cluster_focus(
    91→    items: list[dict],
    92→    plan: dict,
    93→    cluster: str | None,
    94→) -> list[dict]:
    95→    """Filter to only cluster members when a cluster focus is active."""
    96→    effective_cluster = cluster or plan.get("active_cluster")
    97→    if not effective_cluster:
    98→        return items
    99→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   100→    cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   101→    cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   102→    if not cluster_member_ids:
   103→        return items
   104→    return [item for item in items if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   105→
   106→
   107→def stamp_positions(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> None:
   108→    """Stamp queue_position and plan_skipped metadata on each item."""
   109→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   110→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   111→
   112→    for position, item in enumerate(items):
   113→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
   114→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   115→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   116→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   117→            if skip_entry:
   118→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   119→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   120→                if skip_reason:
   121→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   122→
   123→
   124→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   125→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   126→    try:
   127→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   128→
   129→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   130→        if meta:
   131→            return meta.action_type
   132→    except ImportError as exc:
   133→        _ = exc
   134→    return "manual_fix"
   135→
   136→
   137→def _build_cluster_meta(
   138→    cluster_name: str, members: list[dict], cluster_data: dict
   139→) -> dict:
   140→    """Build a cluster meta-item from its member items."""
   141→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   142→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   143→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   144→        action_type = "auto_fix"
   145→    elif "desloppify move" in action:
   146→        action_type = "reorganize"
   147→    else:
   148→        action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   149→        if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify autofix" not in action:
   150→            action_type = "refactor"
   151→
   152→    stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   153→    total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   154→    if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   155→        summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   156→    else:
   157→        summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   158→
   159→    primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   160→    if not primary_command:
   161→        primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   162→
   163→    estimated_impact = max(
   164→        (m.get("estimated_impact", 0.0) for m in members), default=0.0
   165→    )
   166→
   167→    return {
   168→        "id": cluster_name,
   169→        "kind": "cluster",
   170→        "action_type": action_type,
   171→        "summary": summary,
   172→        "members": members,
   173→        "member_count": len(members),
   174→        "primary_command": primary_command,
   175→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   176→        "cluster_auto": True,
   177→        "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   178→        "confidence": "high",
   179→        "detector": detector,
   180→        "file": "",
   181→        "estimated_impact": estimated_impact,
   182→    }
   183→
   184→
   185→def collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   186→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   187→
   188→    Walks the list in order: the first member of each collapsed cluster is
   189→    replaced with its meta-item, subsequent members are skipped.  This
   190→    preserves the ordering established by sort + plan-order.
   191→    """
   192→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   193→    if not clusters:
   194→        return items
   195→
   196→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   197→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   198→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   199→            continue
   200→        for finding_id in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   201→            fid_to_cluster[finding_id] = name
   202→
   203→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   204→        return items
   205→
   206→    # Collect members per cluster (preserving encounter order)
   207→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   208→    for item in items:
   209→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   210→        if cname:
   211→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   212→
   213→    # Build meta-items only for clusters with 2+ members in the queue
   214→    meta_items: dict[str, dict] = {}
   215→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   216→        if len(members) < 2:
   217→            continue
   218→        meta_items[cname] = _build_cluster_meta(
   219→            cname, members, clusters.get(cname, {})
   220→        )
   221→
   222→    # Walk in order: replace first member of each collapsed cluster
   223→    # with its meta-item, skip subsequent members
   224→    seen_clusters: set[str] = set()
   225→    result: list[dict] = []
   226→    for item in items:
   227→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   228→        if cname and cname in meta_items:
   229→            if cname not in seen_clusters:
   230→                seen_clusters.add(cname)
   231→                result.append(meta_items[cname])
   232→            # skip individual member
   233→        else:
   234→            result.append(item)
   235→    return result
   236→
   237→
   238→__all__ = [
   239→    "collapse_clusters",
   240→    "enrich_plan_metadata",
   241→    "filter_cluster_focus",
   242→    "new_item_ids",
   243→    "separate_skipped",
   244→    "stamp_plan_sort_keys",
   245→    "stamp_positions",
   246→]
   247→

1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY,
     7→    detail_dict,
     8→    is_review_finding,
     9→    is_subjective_finding,
    10→    primary_command_for_finding,
    11→    review_finding_weight,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→    slugify,
    14→    status_matches,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import subjective_strict_scores
    18→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    19→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    20→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    21→
    22→# Plan-aware sort tiers (item_sort_key)
    23→_TIER_PLANNED = 0   # Items with explicit plan position
    24→_TIER_EXISTING = 1  # Known items, natural ranking
    25→_TIER_NEW = 2       # Newly discovered items
    26→
    27→# Natural ranking groups (_natural_sort_key)
    28→_RANK_INITIAL_REVIEW = -3  # Unassessed subjective dimensions
    29→_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE = -2    # Epic triage workflow stages
    30→_RANK_WORKFLOW = -1         # Score checkpoints, create-plan
    31→_RANK_CLUSTER = 0           # Auto-clustered findings
    32→_RANK_FINDING = 1           # Individual findings + assessed subjective
    33→
    34→
    35→def enrich_with_impact(items: list[dict], dimension_scores: dict) -> None:
    36→    """Stamp ``estimated_impact`` on each item based on dimension-level headroom.
    37→
    38→    Impact = ``overall_per_point * headroom`` where headroom = ``100 - score``.
    39→    Items in dimensions with more score headroom sort first.
    40→    """
    41→    if not dimension_scores:
    42→        for item in items:
    43→            item["estimated_impact"] = 0.0
    44→        return
    45→
    46→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_breakdown
    47→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import get_dimension_for_detector
    48→
    49→    breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores)
    50→    entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
    51→
    52→    # Build lookup: normalized dimension name -> {per_point, headroom}
    53→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    54→    for entry in entries:
    55→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    56→        if not name:
    57→            continue
    58→        per_point = float(entry.get("overall_per_point", 0.0))
    59→        score = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
    60→        headroom = 100.0 - score
    61→        dim_impact[name.lower()] = {"per_point": per_point, "headroom": headroom}
    62→
    63→    for item in items:
    64→        impact = _compute_item_impact(item, dim_impact, get_dimension_for_detector)
    65→        item["estimated_impact"] = impact
    66→
    67→
    68→def _compute_item_impact(
    69→    item: dict,
    70→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]],
    71→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    72→) -> float:
    73→    """Compute impact value for a single queue item."""
    74→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    75→
    76→    # Subjective items (synthetic dimensions or subjective findings):
    77→    # look up by detail.dimension_name
    78→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    79→        dim_name = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    80→        entry = dim_impact.get(dim_name.lower())
    81→        if entry:
    82→            return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    83→        return 0.0
    84→
    85→    # Mechanical findings: use detector -> dimension mapping
    86→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    87→    if detector:
    88→        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    89→        if dimension:
    90→            entry = dim_impact.get(dimension.name.lower())
    91→            if entry:
    92→                return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    93→
    94→    return 0.0
    95→
    96→
    97→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    98→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    99→        detail = detail_dict(item)
   100→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
   101→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
   102→
   103→
   104→def build_finding_items(
   105→    state: dict,
   106→    *,
   107→    scan_path: str | None,
   108→    status_filter: str,
   109→    scope: str | None,
   110→    chronic: bool,
   111→) -> list[dict]:
   112→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
   113→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
   114→    out: list[dict] = []
   115→
   116→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
   117→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   118→            continue
   119→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
   120→            continue
   121→        if chronic and not (
   122→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
   123→        ):
   124→            continue
   125→
   126→        # Evidence-only: skip findings below standalone confidence threshold
   127→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   128→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   129→        if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
   130→            threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
   131→            finding_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
   132→            if finding_rank > threshold_rank:
   133→                continue
   134→
   135→        item = dict(finding)
   136→        item["id"] = finding_id
   137→        item["kind"] = "finding"
   138→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
   139→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
   140→        item["review_weight"] = (
   141→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
   142→        )
   143→        subjective_score = None
   144→        if item["is_subjective"]:
   145→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
   146→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
   147→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
   148→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
   149→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
   150→            )
   151→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
   152→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
   153→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
   154→            item,
   155→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
   156→        )
   157→
   158→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
   159→            continue
   160→        out.append(item)
   161→
   162→    return out
   163→
   164→
   165→def _natural_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
   166→    """Compute natural (non-plan-aware) ranking for queue items."""
   167→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   168→
   169→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority
   170→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
   171→        return (_RANK_INITIAL_REVIEW, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))
   172→
   173→    # Triage stage items: stage order, blocked after unblocked
   174→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   175→        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
   176→        stage_index = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
   177→        return (_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
   178→
   179→    # Workflow action items (e.g. create-plan)
   180→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   181→        return (_RANK_WORKFLOW, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
   182→
   183→    if kind == "cluster":
   184→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
   185→        action_pri = ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY.get(
   186→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   187→        )
   188→        return (
   189→            _RANK_CLUSTER,
   190→            action_pri,
   191→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   192→            item.get("id", ""),
   193→        )
   194→
   195→    impact = item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0)
   196→
   197→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   198→        return (
   199→            _RANK_FINDING,
   200→            -impact,
   201→            subjective_score_value(item),
   202→            item.get("id", ""),
   203→        )
   204→
   205→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   206→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   207→    return (
   208→        _RANK_FINDING,
   209→        -impact,
   210→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   211→        -review_weight,
   212→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   213→        item.get("id", ""),
   214→    )
   215→
   216→
   217→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
   218→    """Unified sort key: plan position first, then natural ranking.
   219→
   220→    When ``_plan_position`` is stamped (by :func:`stamp_plan_sort_keys`),
   221→    planned items sort first in plan order, then existing items by natural
   222→    ranking, then newly-discovered items by natural ranking.
   223→
   224→    When no plan fields are stamped, falls back to pure natural ranking.
   225→    """
   226→    plan_pos = item.get("_plan_position")
   227→
   228→    if plan_pos is not None:
   229→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   230→        # Triage stages: even when explicitly planned, maintain the
   231→        # blocked-before-unblocked invariant for correctness.
   232→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
   233→            blocked = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
   234→            stage_idx = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
   235→            return (_TIER_PLANNED, 1, blocked, stage_idx, item.get("id", ""))
   236→        # Workflow actions (score communication, create-plan) sort before
   237→        # triage stages so the user sees score context first.
   238→        if kind == "workflow_action":
   239→            return (_TIER_PLANNED, 0, 0, plan_pos, item.get("id", ""))
   240→        return (_TIER_PLANNED, 2, 0, plan_pos, item.get("id", ""))
   241→
   242→    is_new = item.get("_is_new", False)
   243→    group = _TIER_NEW if is_new else _TIER_EXISTING
   244→    return (group, *_natural_sort_key(item))
   245→
   246→
   247→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   248→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   249→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   250→        return {
   251→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   252→            "stage": item.get("stage_name"),
   253→            "is_blocked": item.get("is_blocked", False),
   254→            "blocked_by": item.get("blocked_by", []),
   255→            "policy": "Triage stages sort by dependency order; blocked stages follow unblocked.",
   256→            "ranking_factors": ["blocked_penalty asc", "stage_index asc"],
   257→        }
   258→
   259→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   260→        return {
   261→            "kind": "workflow_action",
   262→            "policy": "Workflow items sort before triage stages and findings.",
   263→            "ranking_factors": ["id asc"],
   264→        }
   265→
   266→    if kind == "cluster":
   267→        return {
   268→            "kind": "cluster",
   269→            "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   270→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
   271→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", 0),
   272→            "policy": "Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type then size.",
   273→            "ranking_factors": ["action_type asc", "member_count desc", "id asc"],
   274→        }
   275→
   276→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   277→        initial = item.get("initial_review", False)
   278→        return {
   279→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   280→            "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   281→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   282→            "initial_review": initial,
   283→            "policy": (
   284→                "Initial review items sort first (onboarding priority)."
   285→                if initial else
   286→                "Sorted by dimension impact (score headroom × weight), then subjective score."
   287→            ),
   288→            "ranking_factors": ["estimated_impact desc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   289→        }
   290→
   291→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   292→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   293→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   294→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   295→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   296→    if is_subjective:
   297→        ranking_factors = ["estimated_impact desc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   298→    elif is_review:
   299→        ranking_factors = [
   300→            "estimated_impact desc",
   301→            "confidence asc",
   302→            "review_weight desc",
   303→            "count desc",
   304→            "id asc",
   305→        ]
   306→    else:
   307→        ranking_factors = ["estimated_impact desc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   308→    explain = {
   309→        "kind": "finding",
   310→        "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   311→        "confidence": confidence,
   312→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   313→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   314→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   315→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   316→    }
   317→    if is_review:
   318→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   319→    if is_subjective:
   320→        explain["policy"] = (
   321→            "Sorted by dimension impact (score headroom × weight), then subjective score."
   322→        )
   323→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   324→    return explain
   325→
   326→
   327→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   328→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   329→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   330→    for item in items:
   331→        if group == "file":
   332→            key = item.get("file", "")
   333→        elif group == "detector":
   334→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   335→        elif group == "cluster":
   336→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   337→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   338→        else:
   339→            key = "items"
   340→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   341→    return grouped
   342→
   343→
   344→__all__ = [
   345→    "build_finding_items",
   346→    "enrich_with_impact",
   347→    "item_explain",
   348→    "item_sort_key",
   349→    "subjective_score_value",
   350→    "group_queue_items",
   351→]
   352→

1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    ExecutionLogEntry,
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    empty_plan,
    10→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    13→
    14→
    15→_DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES = 10000
    16→
    17→
    18→def _get_log_cap() -> int:
    19→    """Read execution_log_max_entries from config. Returns 0 for unlimited."""
    20→    try:
    21→        from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    22→
    23→        config = load_config()
    24→        value = config.get("execution_log_max_entries", _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES)
    25→        return max(0, int(value))
    26→    except (ImportError, OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
    27→        return _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES
    28→
    29→
    30→def append_log_entry(
    31→    plan: PlanModel,
    32→    action: str,
    33→    *,
    34→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    35→    cluster_name: str | None = None,
    36→    actor: str = "user",
    37→    note: str | None = None,
    38→    detail: dict | None = None,
    39→) -> None:
    40→    """Append a structured entry to the plan's execution log."""
    41→    log = plan.get("execution_log", [])
    42→    entry: ExecutionLogEntry = {
    43→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    44→        "action": action,
    45→        "finding_ids": finding_ids or [],
    46→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
    47→        "actor": actor,
    48→        "note": note,
    49→        "detail": detail or {},
    50→    }
    51→    log.append(entry)
    52→    cap = _get_log_cap()
    53→    if cap > 0 and len(log) > cap:
    54→        plan["execution_log"] = log[-cap:]
    55→
    56→
    57→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    58→# Helpers
    59→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    60→
    61→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    62→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    63→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    64→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    65→    if finding_id in order:
    66→        order.remove(finding_id)
    67→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    68→
    69→
    70→def _resolve_position(
    71→    order: list[str],
    72→    position: str,
    73→    target: str | None = None,
    74→    offset: int | None = None,
    75→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    76→) -> int:
    77→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    78→
    79→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    80→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    81→    """
    82→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    83→
    84→    if position == "top":
    85→        return 0
    86→    if position == "bottom":
    87→        return len(order)
    88→
    89→    if position in {"before", "after"} and target:
    90→        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)
    91→
    92→    if position in {"up", "down"} and offset is not None:
    93→        return _resolve_offset_position(
    94→            order,
    95→            moving,
    96→            position=position,
    97→            offset=offset,
    98→            finding_ids=finding_ids,
    99→        )
   100→
   101→    return len(order)
   102→
   103→
   104→def _resolve_relative_position(
   105→    order: list[str],
   106→    moving: set[str],
   107→    *,
   108→    position: str,
   109→    target: str,
   110→) -> int:
   111→    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
   112→        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
   113→            return i if position == "before" else i + 1
   114→    return 0 if position == "before" else len(order)
   115→
   116→
   117→def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:
   118→    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):
   119→        if item_id == target:
   120→            return i
   121→    return None
   122→
   123→
   124→def _resolve_offset_position(
   125→    order: list[str],
   126→    moving: set[str],
   127→    *,
   128→    position: str,
   129→    offset: int,
   130→    finding_ids: list[str] | None,
   131→) -> int:
   132→    if not finding_ids:
   133→        return 0 if position == "up" else len(order)
   134→    first_id = finding_ids[0]
   135→    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
   136→    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)
   137→    if current_idx is None:
   138→        if position == "up":
   139→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
   140→        return len(clean_order)
   141→    if position == "up":
   142→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
   143→    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
   144→
   145→
   146→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   147→# Move items
   148→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   149→
   150→def move_items(
   151→    plan: PlanModel,
   152→    finding_ids: list[str],
   153→    position: str,
   154→    target: str | None = None,
   155→    offset: int | None = None,
   156→) -> int:
   157→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   158→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   159→
   160→    # Triage stage IDs are workflow-managed and cannot be manually reordered
   161→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import TRIAGE_IDS
   162→    finding_ids = [fid for fid in finding_ids if fid not in TRIAGE_IDS]
   163→    if not finding_ids:
   164→        return 0
   165→
   166→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   167→
   168→    # Remove from skipped if present
   169→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   170→    for fid in finding_ids:
   171→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   172→
   173→    # Remove from current position in order
   174→    for fid in finding_ids:
   175→        if fid in order:
   176→            order.remove(fid)
   177→
   178→    # Resolve insertion point
   179→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   180→
   181→    # Insert in original order
   182→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   183→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   184→
   185→    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.
   186→    # Moving to bottom/down/after should not create a promotion barrier.
   187→    if position == "top":
   188→        promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   189→        existing_promoted = set(promoted)
   190→        for fid in finding_ids:
   191→            if fid not in existing_promoted:
   192→                promoted.append(fid)
   193→                existing_promoted.add(fid)
   194→        plan["promoted_ids"] = promoted
   195→
   196→    return len(finding_ids)
   197→
   198→
   199→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   200→# Skip / unskip
   201→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   202→
   203→def skip_items(
   204→    plan: PlanModel,
   205→    finding_ids: list[str],
   206→    *,
   207→    kind: str = "temporary",
   208→    reason: str | None = None,
   209→    note: str | None = None,
   210→    attestation: str | None = None,
   211→    review_after: int | None = None,
   212→    scan_count: int = 0,
   213→) -> int:
   214→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   215→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   216→    now = utc_now()
   217→    count = 0
   218→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   219→    skip_set = set(finding_ids)
   220→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   221→    plan["promoted_ids"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in skip_set]
   222→    for fid in finding_ids:
   223→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   224→        skipped[fid] = {
   225→            "finding_id": fid,
   226→            "kind": kind,
   227→            "reason": reason,
   228→            "note": note,
   229→            "attestation": attestation,
   230→            "created_at": now,
   231→            "review_after": review_after,
   232→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   233→        }
   234→        count += 1
   235→    return count
   236→
   237→
   238→def unskip_items(
   239→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   240→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   241→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   242→
   243→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   244→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   245→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   246→    """
   247→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   248→    count = 0
   249→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   250→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   251→    for fid in finding_ids:
   252→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   253→        if entry is not None:
   254→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   255→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   256→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   257→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   258→            count += 1
   259→    return count, need_reopen
   260→
   261→
   262→def resurface_stale_skips(
   263→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   264→) -> list[str]:
   265→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   266→
   267→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   268→    """
   269→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   270→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   271→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   272→    for fid in list(skipped):
   273→        entry = skipped[fid]
   274→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   275→            continue
   276→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   277→        if review_after is None:
   278→            continue
   279→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   280→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   281→            skipped.pop(fid)
   282→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   283→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   284→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   285→    return resurfaced
   286→
   287→
   288→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   289→# Describe / annotate
   290→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   291→
   292→def describe_finding(
   293→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   294→) -> None:
   295→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   296→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   297→    now = utc_now()
   298→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   299→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   300→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   301→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   302→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   303→
   304→
   305→def annotate_finding(
   306→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   307→) -> None:
   308→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   309→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   310→    now = utc_now()
   311→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   312→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   313→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   314→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   315→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   316→
   317→
   318→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   319→# Reset
   320→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   321→
   322→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   323→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp.
   324→
   325→    Sets ``plan_start_scores`` to a sentinel so the next scan seeds real
   326→    scores instead of incorrectly treating the reset as a completed cycle.
   327→    """
   328→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   329→    plan.clear()
   330→    for k, v in empty_plan().items():
   331→        plan[k] = v
   332→    plan["created"] = created
   333→    # Sentinel: truthy so ``not plan.get("plan_start_scores")`` is False,
   334→    # but recognized by _seed_plan_start_scores as needing replacement.
   335→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {"reset": True}
   336→
   337→
   338→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   339→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   340→
   341→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   342→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   343→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   344→    """
   345→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   346→    found = 0
   347→
   348→    purge_set = set(finding_ids)
   349→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   350→    plan["promoted_ids"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in purge_set]
   351→
   352→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   353→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   354→    for fid in finding_ids:
   355→        was_present = False
   356→        if fid in order:
   357→            order.remove(fid)
   358→            was_present = True
   359→        if fid in skipped:
   360→            skipped.pop(fid)
   361→            was_present = True
   362→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   363→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   364→            if fid in ids:
   365→                ids.remove(fid)
   366→                was_present = True
   367→        # Clear stale cluster reference from override (preserve notes/descriptions)
   368→        override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   369→        if override and override.get("cluster"):
   370→            override["cluster"] = None
   371→            override["updated_at"] = utc_now()
   372→        if was_present:
   373→            found += 1
   374→
   375→    return found
   376→
   377→
   378→__all__ = [
   379→    "annotate_finding",
   380→    "append_log_entry",
   381→    "describe_finding",
   382→    "move_items",
   383→    "purge_ids",
   384→    "reset_plan",
   385→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   386→    "skip_items",
   387→    "unskip_items",
   388→]
   389→

1→"""Central command registry for CLI command handler resolution."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→CommandHandler = Callable[[Any], None]
     9→
    10→_COMMAND_HANDLERS: dict[str, CommandHandler] | None = None
    11→
    12→
    13→def _build_handlers() -> dict[str, CommandHandler]:

    15→    from desloppify.app.commands.config_cmd import cmd_config

    39→def _scorecard_subjective(
    40→    state: dict,
    41→    dim_scores: dict,
    42→) -> list[dict]:
    43→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    44→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    45→
    46→
    47→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    48→    state: dict,
    49→    dim_scores: dict,
    50→    *,
    51→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    52→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    53→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    54→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    55→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    56→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    57→            continue
    58→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    59→        if strict_val < threshold:
    60→            low.append(
    61→                (
    62→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    63→                    strict_val,
    64→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    65→                )
    66→            )
    67→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    68→    return low
    69→
    70→
    71→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    72→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    73→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    74→    state = runtime.state

    77→        return

    83→    if config_warning:

    85→
    86→    print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
    87→    _get_items(args, state, config)

    90→def _resolve_cluster_focus(
    91→    plan_data: dict | None,
    92→    *,
    93→    cluster_arg: str | None,
    94→    scope: str | None,
    95→) -> str | None:
    96→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
    97→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
    98→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    99→        if active_cluster:
   100→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   101→    return effective_cluster
   102→
   103→
   104→def _build_next_payload(
   105→    *,
   106→    queue: dict,
   107→    items: list[dict],
   108→    state: dict,
   109→    narrative: dict,
   110→    plan_data: dict | None,
   111→) -> dict:
   112→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   113→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   114→    )
   115→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   116→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   117→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   118→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   119→        state,
   120→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   121→    )
   122→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   123→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   124→    ]
   125→    return payload
   126→
   127→
   128→def _emit_requested_output(
   129→    args,
   130→    payload: dict,
   131→    items: list[dict],
   132→) -> bool:
   133→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   134→    if output_file:
   135→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   136→            output_file,
   137→            payload,
   138→            len(items),
   139→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   140→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   141→        ):
   142→            return True
   143→        raise SystemExit(1)
   144→
   145→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
   146→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   147→        return True
   148→    return False
   149→
   150→
   151→def _plan_queue_context(
   152→    *,
   153→    state: dict,
   154→    plan_data: dict | None,
   155→    context=None,
   156→) -> tuple[float | None, QueueBreakdown | None]:
   157→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else plan_data
   158→    plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(effective_plan)
   159→    try:
   160→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan_data, context=context)
   161→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   162→        breakdown = None
   163→    return plan_start_strict, breakdown
   164→
   165→
   166→def _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials: dict | None) -> dict | None:
   167→    try:
   168→        return merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   169→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   170→        return raw_potentials or None
   171→
   172→
   173→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
   174→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
   175→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
   176→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
   177→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
   178→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
   179→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   180→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   181→
   182→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
   183→
   184→    # Load the living plan
   185→    plan = load_plan()
   186→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   187→    if (
   188→        plan.get("queue_order")
   189→        or plan.get("overrides")
   190→        or plan.get("clusters")
   191→    ):
   192→        plan_data = plan
   193→
   194→    # Build unified context once — all downstream consumers agree on
   195→    # plan, target_strict, and subjective visibility policy.
   196→    ctx = queue_context(
   197→        state, config=config, plan=plan_data, target_strict=target_strict,
   198→    )
   199→
   200→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   201→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   202→        plan_data,
   203→        cluster_arg=cluster_arg,
   204→        scope=scope,
   205→    )
   206→
   207→    queue = build_work_queue(
   208→        state,
   209→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   210→            count=count,
   211→            scope=scope,
   212→            status=status,
   213→            include_subjective=True,
   214→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   215→            explain=explain,
   216→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   217→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   218→            context=ctx,
   219→        ),
   220→    )
   221→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   222→
   223→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   224→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   225→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   226→        state,
   227→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   228→    )
   229→
   230→    payload = _build_next_payload(
   231→        queue=queue,
   232→        items=items,
   233→        state=state,
   234→        narrative=narrative,
   235→        plan_data=plan_data,
   236→    )
   237→    write_query(payload)
   238→
   239→    if _emit_requested_output(args, payload, items):
   240→        return
   241→
   242→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   243→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   244→        state.get("findings", {}),
   245→        state.get("scan_path"),
   246→    )
   247→
   248→    # Extract frozen plan-start score and queue breakdown for lifecycle display
   249→    plan_start_strict, breakdown = _plan_queue_context(
   250→        state=state,
   251→        plan_data=plan_data,
   252→        context=ctx,
   253→    )
   254→    queue_total = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   255→
   256→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   257→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   258→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(
   259→        queue,
   260→        strict_score,
   261→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   262→        target_strict=target_strict,
   263→    ):
   264→        return
   265→
   266→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   267→    potentials = _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials)
   268→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   269→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   270→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   271→        cluster_filter=effective_cluster,
   272→    )
   273→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   274→    next_render_mod.render_uncommitted_reminder(plan_data)
   275→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   276→        state,
   277→        dim_scores,
   278→        findings_scoped,

   281→        queue_total=queue_total,
   282→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   283→        breakdown=breakdown,

   285→    print()

    54→def cmd_status(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    55→    """Show score dashboard."""
    56→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    57→    state = runtime.state
    58→    config = runtime.config
    59→
    60→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
    61→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    62→    scorecard_dims = scorecard_dimensions_payload(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    63→    subjective_measures = [row for row in scorecard_dims if row.get("subjective")]
    64→    suppression = state_mod.suppression_metrics(state)
    65→
    66→    if getattr(args, "json", False):
    67→        print(
    68→            json.dumps(
    69→                _status_json_payload(
    70→                    state,
    71→                    stats,
    72→                    dim_scores,
    73→                    scorecard_dims,
    74→                    subjective_measures,
    75→                    suppression,

    78→            )
    79→        )
    80→        return
    81→
    82→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    83→        return
    84→
    85→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    86→    if skill_warning:

    88→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    89→    if config_warning:
    90→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    91→
    92→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    93→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
    94→    target_strict_score = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    95→
    96→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    97→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    98→
    99→    # Load living plan for plan-aware rendering and narrative
   100→    _plan = load_plan()
   101→    _plan_active = _plan if (
   102→        _plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")
   103→    ) else None
   104→
   105→    print_triage_guardrail_info(plan=_plan, state=state)
   106→
   107→    narrative = compute_narrative(

   110→    )

   113→    # Build unified context once — plan, target_strict, and policy all agree.

   116→        config=config,
   117→        plan=_plan_active,
   118→        target_strict=target_strict_score,
   119→    )
   120→
   121→    _breakdown = _print_score_section(state, scores, _plan, target_strict_score, _ctx)
   122→    print_scan_metrics(state)

   124→    print_scan_completeness(state)
   125→
   126→    _objective_backlog = _ctx.policy.objective_count
   127→
   128→    if dim_scores:

   130→        reporting_dimensions_mod.show_score_model_breakdown(

   148→
   149→    # Commit tracking reminder

   152→        render_uncommitted_reminder(_plan_active)
   153→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   154→        _logger.debug("commit tracking reminder skipped", exc_info=True)
   155→
   156→    show_agent_plan(narrative, plan=_plan_active)
   157→
   158→    if narrative.get("headline"):

   160→        print()
   161→
   162→    if ignores:

   164→

   166→    if review_age != 30:

   168→        print(colorize(f"  Review staleness: {label}", "dim"))

   189→def _print_score_section(state, scores, plan, target_strict_score, ctx=None):
   190→    """Print score header: frozen plan-start or live score with queue breakdown."""
   191→    plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan)
   192→    breakdown = None
   193→    queue_remaining = 0
   194→    if plan_start_strict is not None:
   195→        try:
   196→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan, context=ctx)
   197→            queue_remaining = breakdown.queue_total
   198→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   199→            logging.debug("Plan-aware queue count failed: %s", exc)
   200→            queue_remaining = 0
   201→    if plan_start_strict is not None and queue_remaining > 0:
   202→        print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   203→            plan, queue_remaining, breakdown=breakdown,
   204→        )
   205→    else:
   206→        for line, style in score_summary_lines(
   207→            overall_score=scores.overall,
   208→            objective_score=scores.objective,
   209→            strict_score=scores.strict,
   210→            verified_strict_score=scores.verified,
   211→            target_strict=target_strict_score,
   212→        ):
   213→            print(colorize(line, style))
   214→        # Show queue breakdown even without frozen score
   215→        if breakdown is None:
   216→            try:
   217→                breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan, context=ctx)
   218→            except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   219→                breakdown = None
   220→        if breakdown is not None and breakdown.queue_total > 0:
   221→            block = format_queue_block(breakdown)
   222→            for text, style in block:
   223→                print(colorize(text, style))
   224→    return breakdown
   225→
   226→
   227→def _status_json_payload(
   228→    state: dict,
   229→    stats: dict,

     2→
     3→Builds workflow stage items, score checkpoint items, create-plan items,
     4→subjective dimension items, and subjective score lookups.

    24→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25→# Dimension key normalization
    26→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27→
    28→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    29→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    30→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    31→    target = cleaned.lower()
    32→
    33→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    34→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    35→            return str(dim_key)
    36→    return slugify(cleaned)
    37→
    38→
    39→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    40→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
    41→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    42→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    43→    return {
    44→        cleaned.lower(),
    45→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    46→        slugify(cleaned),
    47→        canonical.lower(),
    48→        slugify(canonical),
    49→    }
    50→
    51→
    52→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    53→# Subjective strict scores
    54→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    55→
    56→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
    57→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    58→    if not dim_scores:
    59→        return {}
    60→
    61→    entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    62→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
    63→    for entry in entries:
    64→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    65→        if not name:
    66→            continue
    67→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    68→        [REDACTED](name)
    69→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
    70→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
    71→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
    72→            if not key:
    73→                continue
    74→            aliases.add(key)
    75→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
    76→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
    77→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
    78→        for alias in aliases:
    79→            scores[alias] = strict_val
    80→    return scores

   132→        )
   133→

   135→        if name == "commit":

1→"""Terminal rendering helpers for `desloppify autofix --dry-run`."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import random
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→
    12→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    13→
    14→
    15→def show_fix_dry_run_samples(entries: list[dict], results: list[dict]) -> None:
    16→    """Print sampled before/after context for fix --dry-run."""
    17→    random.seed(42)
    18→    print(colorize("\n  ── Sample changes (before → after) ──", "cyan"))
    19→    for result in random.sample(results, min(5, len(results))):
    20→        _print_fix_file_sample(result, entries)
    21→    removed_count = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
    22→    if len(entries) > removed_count:
    23→        print(colorize(f"\n  Note: {len(entries) - removed_count} of {len(entries)} entries were skipped (complex patterns, rest elements, etc.)", "dim"))
    24→    print()
    25→
    26→
    27→def _print_fix_file_sample(result: dict, entries: list[dict]) -> None:
    28→    filepath, removed_set = result["file"], set(result["removed"])
    29→    try:
    30→        path = Path(filepath) if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else Path(".") / filepath
    31→        lines = path.read_text().splitlines()
    32→    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
    33→        _logger.debug("dry-run sample skipped for %s: %s", filepath, exc)
    34→        return
    35→
    36→    file_entries = [entry for entry in entries if entry["file"] == filepath and entry.get("name", "") in removed_set]
    37→    shown = 0
    38→    for entry in file_entries[:2]:
    39→        line_idx = entry.get("line", entry.get("detail", {}).get("line", 0)) - 1
    40→        if line_idx < 0 or line_idx >= len(lines):
    41→            continue
    42→        if shown == 0:
    43→            print(colorize(f"\n  {rel(filepath)}:", "cyan"))
    44→        name = entry.get("name", entry.get("summary", "?"))
    45→        ctx_s, ctx_e = max(0, line_idx - 1), min(len(lines), line_idx + 2)
    46→        print(colorize(f"    {name} (line {line_idx + 1}):", "dim"))
    47→        for idx in range(ctx_s, ctx_e):
    48→            marker = colorize("  →", "red") if idx == line_idx else "   "
    49→            print(f"    {marker} {idx+1:4d}  {lines[idx][:90]}")
    50→        shown += 1
    51→

    21→def _resolve_detector_key(
    22→    detector: str, detect_commands: dict[str, object]
    23→) -> str | None:
    24→    """Resolve detector input to a command key."""
    25→    detector = detector.strip()
    26→    if detector in detect_commands:
    27→        return detector
    28→
    29→    normalized = detector.lower().replace("-", "_")
    30→    if normalized in detect_commands:
    31→        return normalized
    32→
    33→    denormalized = detector.lower().replace("_", "-")
    34→    if denormalized in detect_commands:
    35→        return denormalized
    36→
    37→    return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def cmd_detect(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    41→    """Run a single detector directly (bypass state tracking)."""
    42→    detector_input = args.detector
    43→
    44→    # Resolve language (from --lang flag or auto-detection)
    45→    lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
    46→
    47→    if not lang_cfg:
    48→        langs = ", ".join(lang_api.available_langs()) or "registered language plugins"
    49→        raise CommandError(
    50→            f"No language specified. Use --lang <name> (available: {langs})."

1→"""Shared exception tuples used across command/render flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→
     6→class CommandError(Exception):
     7→    """Raised by command helpers to signal a user-facing CLI error.
     8→
     9→    The CLI entrypoint catches this and prints ``message`` to stderr
    10→    before exiting with ``exit_code``.  Helpers should raise this instead
    11→    of calling ``sys.exit()`` directly so that the error path is testable
    12→    and composable.
    13→    """
    14→
    15→    def __init__(self, message: str, *, exit_code: int = 1) -> None:
    16→        self.message = message
    17→        self.exit_code = exit_code
    18→        super().__init__(message)
    19→
    20→
    21→PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS = (
    22→    ImportError,
    23→    AttributeError,
    24→    OSError,
    25→    ValueError,
    26→    TypeError,
    27→    KeyError,
    28→)
    29→
    30→__all__ = ["CommandError", "PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS"]

    14→SKILL_VERSION_RE = re.compile(r"<!--\s*desloppify-skill-version:\s*(\d+)\s*-->")
    15→SKILL_OVERLAY_RE = re.compile(r"<!--\s*desloppify-overlay:\s*(\w+)\s*-->")
    16→
    17→SKILL_BEGIN = "<!-- desloppify-begin -->"
    18→SKILL_END = "<!-- desloppify-end -->"

    20→# Locations where the skill doc might be installed, relative to project root.
    21→SKILL_SEARCH_PATHS = (
    22→    ".claude/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md",
    23→    ".opencode/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md",
    24→    "AGENTS.md",
    25→    "CLAUDE.md",
    26→    ".cursor/rules/desloppify.md",
    27→    ".github/copilot-instructions.md",
    28→)

    35→    "opencode": (".opencode/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md", "OPENCODE", True),
    36→    "codex": ("AGENTS.md", "CODEX", False),
    37→    "cursor": (".cursor/rules/desloppify.md", "CURSOR", True),
    38→    "copilot": (".github/copilot-instructions.md", "COPILOT", False),
    39→    "windsurf": ("AGENTS.md", "WINDSURF", False),
    40→    "gemini": ("AGENTS.md", "GEMINI", False),
    41→}
    42→
    43→
    44→@dataclass
    45→class SkillInstall:
    46→    """Detected skill document installation."""
    47→
    48→    rel_path: str
    49→    version: int
    50→    overlay: str | None
    51→    stale: bool
    52→
    53→
    54→def find_installed_skill() -> SkillInstall | None:
    55→    """Find installed skill document metadata, or None."""
    56→    project_root = get_project_root()
    57→    for rel_path in SKILL_SEARCH_PATHS:
    58→        full = project_root / rel_path
    59→        if not full.is_file():
    60→            continue
    61→        try:
    62→            content = full.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    63→        except OSError as exc:
    64→            _ = exc
    65→            continue
    66→        version_match = SKILL_VERSION_RE.search(content)
    67→        if not version_match:
    68→            continue
    69→        installed_version = int(version_match.group(1))
    70→        overlay_match = SKILL_OVERLAY_RE.search(content)
    71→        overlay = overlay_match.group(1) if overlay_match else None
    72→        return SkillInstall(
    73→            rel_path=rel_path,
    74→            version=installed_version,
    75→            overlay=overlay,
    76→            stale=installed_version < SKILL_VERSION,
    77→        )
    78→    return None
    79→
    80→

Now let me compile the final report with all the findings I've discovered:

## Summary of Aesthetic Warts in Desloppify Codebase

I've completed a thorough sweep of the desloppify codebase. Here are the specific aesthetic issues I found, organized by category:

### 1. **Inconsistent Logger Usage**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/status_cmd.py`
- **Lines:** 9 defines `_logger` but line 199 uses `logging.debug()` directly
- **Issue:** File defines `_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)` on line 9, but then uses `logging.debug()` on line 199 instead of `_logger.debug()`. This is inconsistent with the initialization.

### 2. **Missing Blank Line After Function**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/cli.py`
- **Lines:** 45-47
- **Issue:** Line 45 has `on_detector_registered(_invalidate_detector_names_cache)` with no blank line before the function definition on line 47. Should have a blank line between module-level statement and function definition per PEP 8.

### 3. **Stale/Re-exported Items in __all__**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/status_parts/render.py`
- **Lines:** 310 in __all__ list
- **Issue:** `show_ignore_summary` is listed in __all__ (line 310) but the function is actually defined in `render_io.py` (line 108 of render_io.py), not in render.py. It's re-exported but not defined locally. All other functions in __all__ are actually defined in render.py except this one.

### 4. **Stale Decorator Comment**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py`
- **Line:** 232
- **Issue:** Comment `# skip individual member` is somewhat obvious - it's placed right before an `else:` that appends to result. The context is already clear from the surrounding code structure.

### 5. **Unused Variable Assignment Pattern**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py`
- **Line:** 133
- **Issue:** `_ = exc` pattern used to suppress linter warnings about unused exception variable. While this is valid Python, it's used in 15+ places across the codebase. This is a stylistic inconsistency - some files use `_ = exc`, others just `except ... as exc:` without the assignment.

---

### Additional Context on Patterns Found:

**Logger Inconsistency Pattern:** 
Multiple files in `app/commands/` have similar issues:
- `_show_terminal.py` line 12: Defines `_logger` (correct)
- `langs.py` line 15: Defines `logger` (inconsistent naming)
- `status_cmd.py` line 199: Uses `logging.debug()` directly despite having `_logger` defined
- `resolve/render.py` line 236: Uses `logging.getLogger(__name__).debug()` inline
- Multiple scan files use `logging.debug()` directly despite importing logging

**Trailing Comma Pattern:**
The code in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` lines 293-301 has consistent trailing commas in dict literals which is good and consistent.

**Blank Line Pattern:**
The codebase generally follows PEP 8 blank line rules well, but the one instance in cli.py at line 45-47 is the main violation.

These are subtle aesthetic issues that don't affect functionality but make the code slightly less polished. The logger inconsistency is the most widespread and would benefit from standardization.

4. **Re-export chains that go nowhere** — things imported and re-exported through __init__.py or facade modules but never actually consumed by end callers.

5. **Backward-compat shims with zero callers** — functions/imports explicitly marked as backward-compatible (look for "compat", "legacy", "deprecated", "shim", "alias", "backwards") and verify they have callers.

6. **Test files that test deleted functionality** — test files whose imports point to modules that no longer exist or functions that were removed.

7. **Config/settings keys that nothing reads** — look in config handling code for keys that are defined/documented but never queried.

8. **Dead branches in if/elif chains** — conditions that can never be true given the current code flow.

9. **Modules in __init__.py __all__ that serve no external purpose** — things exported from package __init__.py that nothing outside the package imports.

10. **Entry points or CLI subcommands that are registered but broken or vestigial**.

1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
     9→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    10→    compute_subjective_visibility,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import QueueContext
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    14→    ALL_STATUSES,
    15→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    16→    scope_matches,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import (
    19→    build_communicate_score_item,
    20→    build_create_plan_item,
    21→    build_import_scores_item,
    22→    build_subjective_items,
    23→    build_triage_stage_items,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    26→    build_finding_items,
    27→    enrich_with_impact,
    28→    group_queue_items,
    29→    item_explain,
    30→    item_sort_key,
    31→)
    32→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import (
    33→    collapse_clusters,
    34→    enrich_plan_metadata,
    35→    filter_cluster_focus,
    36→    new_item_ids as _new_item_ids,
    37→    separate_skipped,
    38→    stamp_plan_sort_keys,
    39→    stamp_positions,
    40→)
    41→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    42→
    43→# Sentinel: "read scan_path from state" (the safe default).
    44→# Callers that want to override can pass an explicit str or None.
    45→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE = object()
    46→
    47→
    48→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    49→class QueueBuildOptions:
    50→    """Configuration for queue construction.
    51→
    52→    ``scan_path`` defaults to reading from ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers
    53→    don't need to thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` or ``None``
    54→    to override (``None`` disables scope filtering).
    55→    """
    56→
    57→    # Output control
    58→    count: int | None = 1
    59→    explain: bool = False
    60→
    61→    # Scope filtering
    62→    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
    63→    scope: str | None = None
    64→    status: str = "open"
    65→    chronic: bool = False
    66→
    67→    # Subjective gating
    68→    include_subjective: bool = True
    69→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    70→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None
    71→
    72→    # Plan integration
    73→    plan: dict | None = None
    74→    include_skipped: bool = False
    75→    cluster: str | None = None
    76→
    77→    # Pre-computed context (overrides plan/policy)
    78→    context: QueueContext | None = None
    79→
    80→
    81→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    82→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    83→
    84→    items: list[dict]
    85→    total: int
    86→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    87→    new_ids: set[str]
    88→
    89→
    90→def build_work_queue(
    91→    state: StateModel,
    92→    *,
    93→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    94→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    95→    """Build a ranked work queue from state findings.
    96→
    97→    Pipeline:
    98→    1. Gather — finding items, subjective dimensions, workflow stages
    99→    2. Score  — estimate impact from dimension headroom, apply floor
   100→    3. Order  — stamp plan positions, sort, filter to cluster focus
   101→    4. Limit  — truncate to count, optionally add explain metadata
   102→    """
   103→    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   104→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = _resolve_inputs(opts, state)
   105→
   106→    # 1. Gather
   107→    items = build_finding_items(
   108→        state, scan_path=scan_path, status_filter=status,
   109→        scope=opts.scope, chronic=opts.chronic,
   110→    )
   111→    items += _gather_subjective_items(state, opts, plan, threshold)
   112→    items += _gather_workflow_items(state, plan, status)
   113→
   114→    # 2. Score & filter
   115→    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   116→    items = [i for i in items if _passes_impact_floor(i)]
   117→
   118→    # 3. Plan-aware ordering
   119→    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)
   120→    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   121→    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)
   122→
   123→    # 4. Finalize
   124→    if not items:
   125→        items += _empty_queue_fallback(plan)
   126→    total = len(items)
   127→    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
   128→        items = items[:opts.count]
   129→    if opts.explain:
   130→        for item in items:
   131→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   132→
   133→    return {
   134→        "items": items,
   135→        "total": total,
   136→        "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"),
   137→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   138→    }
   139→
   140→
   141→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   142→# Pipeline helpers (private to this module)
   143→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   144→
   145→
   146→def _resolve_inputs(
   147→    opts: QueueBuildOptions, state: StateModel,
   148→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None, str, float]:
   149→    """Resolve plan, scan_path, status, and subjective threshold from options."""
   150→    ctx = opts.context
   151→    plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else opts.plan
   152→
   153→    scan_path: str | None = (
   154→        state.get("scan_path")
   155→        if opts.scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
   156→        else opts.scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
   157→    )
   158→
   159→    status = opts.status
   160→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   161→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   162→
   163→    try:
   164→        threshold = float(opts.subjective_threshold)
   165→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   166→        threshold = 100.0
   167→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, threshold))
   168→
   169→    return plan, scan_path, status, threshold
   170→
   171→
   172→def _gather_subjective_items(
   173→    state: StateModel,
   174→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   175→    plan: dict | None,
   176→    threshold: float,
   177→) -> list[dict]:
   178→    """Build synthetic subjective items, gated by SubjectiveVisibility policy."""
   179→    if not opts.include_subjective:
   180→        return []
   181→    if opts.status not in {"open", "all"}:
   182→        return []
   183→    if opts.chronic:
   184→        return []
   185→
   186→    ctx = opts.context
   187→    policy = (
   188→        (ctx.policy if ctx is not None else None)
   189→        or opts.policy
   190→        or compute_subjective_visibility(state, plan=plan)
   191→    )
   192→
   193→    candidates = build_subjective_items(
   194→        state, state.get("findings", {}), threshold=threshold,
   195→    )
   196→
   197→    # When a plan explicitly includes a subjective item in queue_order,
   198→    # surface it regardless of policy — the plan is authoritative.
   199→    plan_queue_set: set[str] = (
   200→        set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   201→        if plan
   202→        else set()
   203→    )
   204→
   205→    result: list[dict] = []
   206→    for item in candidates:
   207→        if not scope_matches(item, opts.scope):
   208→            continue
   209→        item_id = item.get("id", "")
   210→        if not policy.should_surface(item) and item_id not in plan_queue_set:
   211→            continue
   212→        result.append(item)
   213→    return result
   214→
   215→
   216→def _gather_workflow_items(
   217→    state: StateModel, plan: dict | None, status: str,
   218→) -> list[dict]:
   219→    """Inject triage stages, checkpoints, and create-plan when plan is active."""
   220→    if not plan or status not in {"open", "all"}:
   221→        return []
   222→
   223→    items: list[dict] = list(build_triage_stage_items(plan, state))
   224→    for builder in (
   225→        build_import_scores_item,
   226→        build_communicate_score_item,
   227→    ):
   228→        item = builder(plan, state)
   229→        if item is not None:
   230→            items.append(item)
   231→    plan_item = build_create_plan_item(plan)
   232→    if plan_item is not None:
   233→        items.append(plan_item)
   234→    return items
   235→
   236→
   237→_MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT = 0.05
   238→
   239→
   240→def _passes_impact_floor(item: dict) -> bool:
   241→    """Return True if item should survive the impact floor filter."""
   242→    if item.get("kind") != "finding":
   243→        return True
   244→    if item.get("is_review") or item.get("is_subjective"):
   245→        return True
   246→    impact = item.get("estimated_impact")
   247→    return not impact or impact >= _MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT
   248→
   249→
   250→def _plan_presort(
   251→    items: list[dict], state: StateModel, plan: dict | None,
   252→) -> tuple[set[str], list[dict]]:
   253→    """Enrich plan metadata and stamp sort keys before sorting.
   254→
   255→    Returns ``(new_ids, skipped)`` — skipped items are removed from
   256→    ``items`` in place and returned separately for post-sort re-append.
   257→    """
   258→    if not plan:
   259→        return set(), []
   260→
   261→    new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   262→    enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)
   263→    stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids)
   264→    remaining, skipped = separate_skipped(items, plan)
   265→    items[:] = remaining
   266→    return new_ids, skipped
   267→
   268→
   269→def _plan_postsort(
   270→    items: list[dict],
   271→    skipped: list[dict],
   272→    plan: dict | None,
   273→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   274→) -> None:
   275→    """Re-append skipped items, stamp positions, filter to cluster focus."""
   276→    if not plan:
   277→        return
   278→
   279→    if opts.include_skipped:
   280→        items.extend(skipped)
   281→    stamp_positions(items, plan)
   282→    focused = filter_cluster_focus(items, plan, opts.cluster)
   283→    items[:] = focused
   284→
   285→
   286→def _empty_queue_fallback(plan: dict | None) -> list[dict]:
   287→    """Return a 'run scan' nudge when an active plan cycle has cleared."""
   288→    if not plan:
   289→        return []
   290→    plan_scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   291→    if plan_scores.get("strict") is None:
   292→        return []
   293→    return [{
   294→        "id": "workflow::run-scan",
   295→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   296→        "summary": "Queue cleared \u2014 run scan to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   297→        "primary_command": "desloppify scan",
   298→        "file": "",
   299→        "detector": "workflow",
   300→        "confidence": "high",
   301→    }]
   302→
   303→
   304→__all__ = [
   305→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   306→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   307→    "QueueContext",
   308→    "WorkQueueResult",
   309→    "build_work_queue",
   310→    "collapse_clusters",
   311→    "group_queue_items",
   312→]
   313→
