1→"""Predicates and small utilities for work queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    11→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    12→)
    13→
    14→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    15→ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "refactor": 1, "manual_fix": 2, "reorganize": 3}
    16→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    17→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    18→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    19→)
    20→
    21→
    22→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    23→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    24→    detail = item.get("detail")
    25→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    26→
    27→
    28→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    29→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    30→
    31→
    32→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    33→    detector = item.get("detector")
    34→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    35→        return True
    36→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    37→        return True
    38→    return False
    39→
    40→
    41→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    42→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    """True for subjective work items, including collapsed subjective clusters."""
    47→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    48→        return True
    49→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    50→        members = item.get("members", [])
    51→        return bool(members) and all(
    52→            m.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" for m in members
    53→        )
    54→    return False
    55→
    56→
    57→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    58→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    59→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    60→    weight_by_confidence = {
    61→        "high": 1.0,
    62→        "medium": 0.7,
    63→        "low": 0.3,
    64→    }
    65→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    66→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    67→        weight *= 10.0
    68→    return float(weight)
    69→
    70→
    71→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    72→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    73→    if not scope:
    74→        return True
    75→
    76→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    77→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    78→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    79→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    80→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    81→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    82→
    83→    if "*" in scope:
    84→        return any(
    85→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    86→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    87→        )
    88→
    89→    if "::" in scope:
    90→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    91→
    92→    lowered = scope.lower()
    93→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    94→        return (
    95→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    96→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    97→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    98→        )
    99→
   100→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
   101→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
   102→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
   103→
   104→    return (
   105→        detector == scope
   106→        or filepath == scope
   107→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   108→    )
   109→
   110→
   111→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   112→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   113→
   114→
   115→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   116→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   117→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   118→    if not lang:
   119→        return None
   120→
   121→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   122→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   123→        return None
   124→
   125→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   126→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   127→        return None
   128→
   129→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   130→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   131→        return None
   132→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   133→
   134→
   135→def primary_command_for_finding(
   136→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   137→) -> str:
   138→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   139→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   140→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   141→        available_fixers = [
   142→            fixer
   143→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   144→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   145→        ]
   146→        if available_fixers:
   147→            return f"desloppify autofix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   148→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   149→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   150→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   151→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   152→    return f'desloppify plan resolve "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   153→

1→"""State-backed work queue for review findings.
     2→
     3→Review findings live in state["findings"]. This module provides:
     4→- Listing/sorting open review findings by impact
     5→- Storing investigation notes on findings
     6→- Expiring stale holistic findings during scan
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→import logging
    12→from datetime import UTC, datetime
    13→
    14→from desloppify.core.issues_render import finding_weight
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import detail_dict
    16→
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→__all__ = [
    20→    "impact_label",
    21→    "list_open_review_findings",
    22→    "update_investigation",
    23→    "expire_stale_holistic",
    24→]
    25→
    26→
    27→def impact_label(weight: float) -> str:
    28→    """Convert weight to a human-readable impact label."""
    29→    try:
    30→        numeric = float(weight)
    31→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    32→        return "+"
    33→    if numeric >= 8:
    34→        return "+++"
    35→    if numeric >= 5:
    36→        return "++"
    37→    return "+"
    38→
    39→
    40→def list_open_review_findings(state: dict) -> list[dict]:
    41→    """Return open review findings sorted by impact (highest first)."""
    42→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    43→    review = [
    44→        finding
    45→        for finding in findings.values()
    46→        if finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("detector") == "review"
    47→    ]
    48→
    49→    def _sort_key(finding: dict) -> tuple[float, str]:
    50→        weight, _impact, finding_id = finding_weight(finding)
    51→        return (-weight, finding_id)
    52→
    53→    review.sort(key=_sort_key)
    54→    return review
    55→
    56→
    57→def update_investigation(state: dict, finding_id: str, text: str) -> bool:
    58→    """Store investigation text on a finding. Returns False if not found/not open."""
    59→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    60→    if not finding or finding.get("status") != "open":
    61→        return False
    62→    detail = detail_dict(finding)
    63→    if not detail:
    64→        detail = {}
    65→        finding["detail"] = detail
    66→    detail["investigation"] = text
    67→    detail["investigated_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()
    68→    return True
    69→
    70→
    71→def expire_stale_holistic(state: dict, max_age_days: int = 30) -> list[str]:
    72→    """Auto-resolve holistic review findings older than max_age_days."""
    73→    now = datetime.now(UTC)
    74→    expired: list[str] = []
    75→
    76→    for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    77→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    78→            continue
    79→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    80→            continue
    81→        if not detail_dict(finding).get("holistic"):
    82→            continue
    83→
    84→        last_seen = finding.get("last_seen")
    85→        if not last_seen:
    86→            continue
    87→
    88→        try:
    89→            seen_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(last_seen)
    90→        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
    91→            logger.debug(
    92→                "Skipping holistic finding %s with invalid last_seen %r: %s",
    93→                finding_id,
    94→                last_seen,
    95→                exc,
    96→            )
    97→            continue
    98→
    99→        age_days = (now - seen_dt).days
   100→        if age_days > max_age_days:
   101→            finding["status"] = "auto_resolved"
   102→            finding["resolved_at"] = now.isoformat()
   103→            finding["note"] = "holistic review expired — re-run review to re-evaluate"
   104→            expired.append(finding_id)
   105→
   106→    return expired
   107→
```

1→"""Unified queue-resolution context.
     2→
     3→A frozen ``QueueContext`` computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``plan`` / ``target_strict`` / ``policy`` threading through function chains.
     5→Callers build one context and pass it everywhere — makes the wrong thing
     6→impossible.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from dataclasses import dataclass
    12→
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    14→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    15→    compute_subjective_visibility,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→# Sentinel: "auto-load plan from disk" (the default).
    20→_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD = object()
    21→
    22→
    23→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    24→class QueueContext:
    25→    """Immutable snapshot of resolved queue parameters.
    26→
    27→    Built once via :func:`queue_context`, then threaded through
    28→    ``build_work_queue``, ``plan_aware_queue_breakdown``, and command
    29→    helpers so every call site agrees on plan, target, and policy.
    30→    """
    31→
    32→    plan: dict | None
    33→    target_strict: float
    34→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility
    35→
    36→
    37→def queue_context(
    38→    state: StateModel,
    39→    *,
    40→    config: dict | None = None,
    41→    plan: dict | None | object = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    42→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    43→) -> QueueContext:
    44→    """Build a :class:`QueueContext` with all parameters resolved.
    45→
    46→    Resolution order:
    47→
    48→    1. **plan** — explicit value wins; sentinel ``_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD`` triggers
    49→       ``load_plan()`` (guarded by ``PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS``).
    50→    2. **target_strict** — explicit float wins; ``None`` reads from *config*
    51→       via ``target_strict_score_from_config``; final fallback is ``95.0``.
    52→    3. **policy** — ``compute_subjective_visibility(state, ...)`` with the
    53→       resolved plan and target_strict so every downstream consumer sees
    54→       the same objective-vs-subjective balance.
    55→    """
    56→    # --- resolve plan ---
    57→    if plan is _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD:
    58→        from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    59→        from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    60→
    61→        try:
    62→            resolved_plan: dict | None = load_plan()
    63→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    64→            resolved_plan = None
    65→    else:
    66→        resolved_plan = plan  # type: ignore[assignment]
    67→
    68→    # --- resolve target_strict ---
    69→    if target_strict is not None:
    70→        resolved_target = target_strict
    71→    elif config is not None:
    72→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import (
    73→            target_strict_score_from_config,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        resolved_target = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    77→    else:
    78→        resolved_target = 95.0
    79→
    80→    # --- resolve policy ---
    81→    resolved_policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
    82→        state,
    83→        target_strict=resolved_target,
    84→        plan=resolved_plan,
    85→    )
    86→
    87→    return QueueContext(
    88→        plan=resolved_plan,
    89→        target_strict=resolved_target,
    90→        policy=resolved_policy,
    91→    )
    92→
    93→
    94→__all__ = [
    95→    "QueueContext",
    96→    "queue_context",
    97→]
    98→
```

     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY,
     7→    detail_dict,

    17→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import subjective_strict_scores
    18→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    19→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    20→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    21→
    22→# Plan-aware sort tiers (item_sort_key)
    23→_TIER_PLANNED = 0   # Items with explicit plan position
    24→_TIER_EXISTING = 1  # Known items, natural ranking
    25→_TIER_NEW = 2       # Newly discovered items
    26→
    27→# Natural ranking groups (_natural_sort_key)
    28→_RANK_INITIAL_REVIEW = -3  # Unassessed subjective dimensions
    29→_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE = -2    # Epic triage workflow stages
    30→_RANK_WORKFLOW = -1         # Score checkpoints, create-plan
    31→_RANK_CLUSTER = 0           # Auto-clustered findings
    32→_RANK_FINDING = 1           # Individual findings + assessed subjective
    33→
    34→
    35→def enrich_with_impact(items: list[dict], dimension_scores: dict) -> None:
    36→    """Stamp ``estimated_impact`` on each item based on dimension-level headroom.
    37→
    38→    Impact = ``overall_per_point * headroom`` where headroom = ``100 - score``.
    39→    Items in dimensions with more score headroom sort first.
    40→    """
    41→    if not dimension_scores:
    42→        for item in items:
    43→            item["estimated_impact"] = 0.0
    44→        return
    45→
    46→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_breakdown
    47→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import get_dimension_for_detector
    48→
    49→    breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores)
    50→    entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
    51→
    52→    # Build lookup: normalized dimension name -> {per_point, headroom}
    53→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    54→    for entry in entries:
    55→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    56→        if not name:
    57→            continue
    58→        per_point = float(entry.get("overall_per_point", 0.0))
    59→        score = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
    60→        headroom = 100.0 - score
    61→        dim_impact[name.lower()] = {"per_point": per_point, "headroom": headroom}
    62→
    63→    for item in items:
    64→        impact = _compute_item_impact(item, dim_impact, get_dimension_for_detector)
    65→        item["estimated_impact"] = impact
    66→
    67→
    68→def _compute_item_impact(
    69→    item: dict,
    70→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]],
    71→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    72→) -> float:
    73→    """Compute impact value for a single queue item."""
    74→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    75→
    76→    # Subjective items (synthetic dimensions or subjective findings):
    77→    # look up by detail.dimension_name
    78→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    79→        dim_name = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    80→        entry = dim_impact.get(dim_name.lower())
    81→        if entry:
    82→            return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    83→        return 0.0
    84→
    85→    # Mechanical findings: use detector -> dimension mapping
    86→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    87→    if detector:
    88→        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    89→        if dimension:
    90→            entry = dim_impact.get(dimension.name.lower())
    91→            if entry:
    92→                return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    93→
    94→    return 0.0
    95→
    96→
    97→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    98→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    99→        detail = detail_dict(item)
   100→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))

1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.state import StateModel
     6→
     7→
     8→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
     9→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    10→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    11→    if not scan_history:
    12→        return set()
    13→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    14→    if not threshold:
    15→        return set()
    16→    return {
    17→        finding_id
    18→        for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    19→        if finding.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    20→    }
    21→
    22→
    23→def enrich_plan_metadata(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> None:
    24→    """Stamp plan description, note, and cluster info from overrides."""
    25→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    26→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    27→
    28→    for item in items:
    29→        override = overrides.get(item["id"], {})
    30→        if override.get("description"):
    31→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    32→        if override.get("note"):
    33→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    34→        if override.get("cluster"):
    35→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    36→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    37→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    38→                "name": cluster_name,
    39→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    40→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    41→            }
    42→
    43→
    44→def stamp_plan_sort_keys(
    45→    items: list[dict],
    46→    plan: dict,
    47→    new_ids: set[str],
    48→) -> None:
    49→    """Stamp ``_plan_position`` and ``_is_new`` on each item.
    50→
    51→    These fields are consumed by :func:`item_sort_key` in ``ranking.py``
    52→    to produce the correct final ordering in a single sort pass.
    53→    """
    54→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    55→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    56→
    57→    position_map: dict[str, int] = {}
    58→    for idx, finding_id in enumerate(queue_order):
    59→        if finding_id not in skipped_ids:
    60→            position_map[finding_id] = idx
    61→
    62→    for item in items:
    63→        item_id = item["id"]
    64→        pos = position_map.get(item_id)
    65→        item["_plan_position"] = pos  # None if not in queue_order
    66→        item["_is_new"] = item_id in new_ids
    67→
    68→
    69→def separate_skipped(
    70→    items: list[dict],
    71→    plan: dict,
    72→) -> tuple[list[dict], list[dict]]:
    73→    """Separate skipped items from the main list.
    74→
    75→    Returns ``(non_skipped, skipped)`` so callers can optionally re-append.
    76→    """
    77→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    78→    if not skipped_ids:
    79→        return items, []
    80→    non_skipped: list[dict] = []
    81→    skipped: list[dict] = []
    82→    for item in items:
    83→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
    84→            skipped.append(item)
    85→        else:
    86→            non_skipped.append(item)
    87→    return non_skipped, skipped
    88→
    89→
    90→def filter_cluster_focus(
    91→    items: list[dict],
    92→    plan: dict,
    93→    cluster: str | None,
    94→) -> list[dict]:
    95→    """Filter to only cluster members when a cluster focus is active."""
    96→    effective_cluster = cluster or plan.get("active_cluster")
    97→    if not effective_cluster:
    98→        return items
    99→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   100→    cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})

1→"""Synthetic work-queue item builders and dimension scoring.
     2→
     3→Builds workflow stage items, score checkpoint items, create-plan items,
     4→subjective dimension items, and subjective score lookups.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    10→    all_subjective_entries,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    13→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    14→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,

    24→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25→# Dimension key normalization
    26→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27→
    28→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    29→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    30→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    31→    target = cleaned.lower()
    32→
    33→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    34→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    35→            return str(dim_key)
    36→    return slugify(cleaned)
    37→
    38→
    39→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    40→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
    41→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    42→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    43→    return {
    44→        cleaned.lower(),
    45→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    46→        slugify(cleaned),
    47→        canonical.lower(),
    48→        slugify(canonical),
    49→    }
    50→
    51→
    52→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    53→# Subjective strict scores
    54→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    55→
    56→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
    57→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    58→    if not dim_scores:
    59→        return {}
    60→
    61→    entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    62→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
    63→    for entry in entries:
    64→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    65→        if not name:
    66→            continue
    67→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    68→        [REDACTED](name)
    69→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
    70→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
    71→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
    72→            if not key:
    73→                continue
    74→            aliases.add(key)
    75→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
    76→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
    77→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
    78→        for alias in aliases:
    79→            scores[alias] = strict_val
    80→    return scores
    81→

     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→
     6→class CommandError(Exception):
     7→    """Raised by command helpers to signal a user-facing CLI error.
     8→
     9→    The CLI entrypoint catches this and prints ``message`` to stderr
    10→    before exiting with ``exit_code``.  Helpers should raise this instead
    11→    of calling ``sys.exit()`` directly so that the error path is testable
    12→    and composable.
    13→    """
    14→
    15→    def __init__(self, message: str, *, exit_code: int = 1) -> None:
    16→        self.message = message
    17→        self.exit_code = exit_code
    18→        super().__init__(message)

   185→def collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   186→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   187→
   188→    Walks the list in order: the first member of each collapsed cluster is
   189→    replaced with its meta-item, subsequent members are skipped.  This
   190→    preserves the ordering established by sort + plan-order.
   191→    """
   192→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   193→    if not clusters:
   194→        return items
   195→
   196→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   197→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   198→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   199→            continue
   200→        for finding_id in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   201→            fid_to_cluster[finding_id] = name
   202→
   203→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   204→        return items
   205→
   206→    # Collect members per cluster (preserving encounter order)
   207→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   208→    for item in items:
   209→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))

1→"""Score/value accessor helpers for state schema payloads."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→
     9→def json_default(obj: Any) -> Any:
    10→    """JSON serializer fallback for known non-JSON-native state values."""
    11→    if isinstance(obj, set):
    12→        return sorted(obj)
    13→    if isinstance(obj, Path):
    14→        return str(obj).replace("\\", "/")
    15→    if hasattr(obj, "isoformat"):
    16→        return obj.isoformat()
    17→    raise TypeError(
    18→        f"Object of type {type(obj).__name__} is not JSON serializable: {obj!r}"
    19→    )
    20→
    21→
    22→def get_overall_score(state: dict[str, Any]) -> float | None:
    23→    value = state.get("overall_score")
    24→    return float(value) if isinstance(value, int | float) else None
    25→
    26→
    27→def get_objective_score(state: dict[str, Any]) -> float | None:
    28→    value = state.get("objective_score")
    29→    return float(value) if isinstance(value, int | float) else None
    30→
    31→
    32→def get_strict_score(state: dict[str, Any]) -> float | None:
    33→    value = state.get("strict_score")
    34→    return float(value) if isinstance(value, int | float) else None
    35→
    36→
    37→def get_verified_strict_score(state: dict[str, Any]) -> float | None:
    38→    value = state.get("verified_strict_score")
    39→    return float(value) if isinstance(value, int | float) else None
    40→
    41→

    54→

1→"""Runtime compatibility governance matrix and helper predicates.
     2→
     3→This module is the single source of truth for import-boundary policy:
     4→- public runtime modules (stable entry surfaces),
     5→- deprecated compatibility modules (removal candidates),
     6→- private modules (internal implementation details).
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from collections.abc import Iterable
    12→
    13→
    14→# Public package roots intended for runtime imports across the codebase.
    15→PUBLIC_RUNTIME_ROOTS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    16→    "desloppify.app",
    17→    "desloppify.core",
    18→    "desloppify.engine",
    19→    "desloppify.intelligence",
    20→    "desloppify.languages",
    21→)
    22→
    23→# Compatibility shims removed from runtime usage; these must not be reintroduced.
    24→SOFT_DEPRECATED_MODULES: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    25→    {
    26→        "desloppify.utils",
    27→        "desloppify.file_discovery",
    28→        "desloppify.hook_registry",
    29→        "desloppify.search",
    30→        "desloppify.versioning",
    31→        "desloppify.core.output_api",
    32→        "desloppify.core.output_contract",
    33→    }
    34→)
    35→
    36→SOFT_DEPRECATED_SHORT_IMPORTS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    37→    {
    38→        "utils",
    39→        "file_discovery",
    40→        "hook_registry",
    41→        "search",
    42→        "versioning",
    43→    }
    44→)
    45→
    46→# Private internals: only code under these roots may import them directly.
    47→PRIVATE_MODULE_PREFIXES: tuple[str, ...] = ("desloppify.core._internal",)
    48→PRIVATE_ALLOWED_IMPORTER_PREFIXES: tuple[str, ...] = ("desloppify.core",)
    49→
    50→
    51→def is_soft_deprecated_module(module: str) -> bool:
    52→    """Return True when module is a soft-deprecated compatibility surface."""
    53→    return module in SOFT_DEPRECATED_MODULES
    54→
    55→
    56→def is_private_module(module: str) -> bool:
    57→    """Return True when module path belongs to a private internal namespace."""
    58→    normalized = module.strip()
    59→    if not normalized:
    60→        return False
    61→    for prefix in PRIVATE_MODULE_PREFIXES:
    62→        if normalized == prefix or normalized.startswith(f"{prefix}."):
    63→            return True
    64→    return False
    65→
    66→
    67→def importer_can_access_private(importer_module: str) -> bool:
    68→    """Return True when importer module is allowed to reference private APIs."""
    69→    normalized = importer_module.strip()
    70→    if not normalized:
    71→        return False
    72→    for prefix in PRIVATE_ALLOWED_IMPORTER_PREFIXES:
    73→        if normalized == prefix or normalized.startswith(f"{prefix}."):
    74→            return True
    75→    return False
    76→
    77→
    78→def iter_soft_deprecated_module_paths() -> Iterable[str]:
    79→    """Yield python-module relative paths for deprecated compatibility modules."""
    80→    for module in sorted(SOFT_DEPRECATED_MODULES):
    81→        parts = module.split(".")
    82→        yield "/".join(parts) + ".py"
    83→
    84→
    85→__all__ = [
    86→    "PRIVATE_ALLOWED_IMPORTER_PREFIXES",
    87→    "PRIVATE_MODULE_PREFIXES",
    88→    "PUBLIC_RUNTIME_ROOTS",
    89→    "SOFT_DEPRECATED_MODULES",
    90→    "SOFT_DEPRECATED_SHORT_IMPORTS",
    91→    "importer_can_access_private",
    92→    "is_private_module",
    93→    "is_soft_deprecated_module",
    94→    "iter_soft_deprecated_module_paths",
    95→]
    96→

1→"""plan command: generate prioritized markdown plan from state.

     3→This module preserves the original import path. The full plan command

    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    14→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import warn_best_effort
    15→from desloppify.engine import planning as planning_mod
    16→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    17→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    18→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_config_staleness

    21→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    22→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""

    24→    state = runtime.state
    25→

    27→        return
    28→
    29→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(runtime.config)
    30→    if config_warning:

    32→
    33→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    34→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    35→
    36→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    37→    if output:
    38→        try:
    39→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    40→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    41→            print_agent_plan(
    42→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    43→                next_command=next_command,
    44→            )
    45→        except OSError as e:
    46→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    47→    else:
    48→        print(plan_md)
    49→        print()
    50→        print_agent_plan(
    51→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
    52→            next_command=next_command,
    53→        )
    54→
    55→
    56→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_output"]
    57→

     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import sys

     9→
    10→from desloppify.core.config import config_for_query, load_config
    11→from desloppify.core.file_paths import safe_write_text
    12→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    13→from desloppify.state import json_default
    14→
    15→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    16→
    17→QUERY_PAYLOAD_MAX_BYTES = 2_000_000
    18→QUERY_ITEMS_SOFT_LIMIT = 200
    19→QUERY_CLUSTER_MEMBER_LIMIT = 10
    20→QUERY_TEXT_LIMIT = 400
    21→
    22→
    23→def _payload_size_bytes(payload: dict) -> int:
    24→    """Return UTF-8 byte size for a JSON-serializable payload."""
    25→    return len(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, default=json_default).encode("utf-8"))
    26→
    27→
    28→def _truncate_text(value: object, *, limit: int | None = None) -> object:
    29→    """Return text truncated to a fixed character budget."""
    30→    if not isinstance(value, str):
    31→        return value
    32→    limit_value = QUERY_TEXT_LIMIT if limit is None else limit
    33→    if len(value) <= limit_value:
    34→        return value
    35→    return value[: limit_value - 1] + "…"
    36→
    37→
    38→def _lightweight_item(item: object) -> object:
    39→    """Return a lighter-weight queue item safe for query payload persistence."""
    40→    if not isinstance(item, dict):
    41→        return item
    42→
    43→    light = dict(item)
    44→    light["summary"] = _truncate_text(light.get("summary"))
    45→
    46→    detail = light.get("detail")
    47→    if isinstance(detail, dict):
    48→        compact_detail: dict = {}
    49→        for key, value in detail.items():
    50→            if isinstance(value, str):
    51→                compact_detail[key] = _truncate_text(value, limit=200)
    52→            elif isinstance(value, list):
    53→                compact_detail[key] = value[:20]
    54→            elif isinstance(value, dict):
    55→                compact_detail[key] = {"truncated": True, "keys": len(value)}
    56→            else:
    57→                compact_detail[key] = value
    58→        light["detail"] = compact_detail
    59→
    60→    if light.get("kind") == "cluster":
    61→        members = light.get("members")
    62→        if isinstance(members, list) and len(members) > QUERY_CLUSTER_MEMBER_LIMIT:
    63→            light["members"] = members[:QUERY_CLUSTER_MEMBER_LIMIT]
    64→            light["members_truncated"] = True
    65→            light["members_sample_limit"] = QUERY_CLUSTER_MEMBER_LIMIT
    66→
    67→    return light
    68→
    69→
    70→def _minimal_payload(payload: dict, *, max_bytes: int) -> dict:
    71→    """Build a minimal fallback payload when budget cannot be met."""
    72→    minimal: dict = {
    73→        "command": payload.get("command"),
    74→        "queue": payload.get("queue", {}),
    75→        "overall_score": payload.get("overall_score"),
    76→        "objective_score": payload.get("objective_score"),
    77→        "strict_score": payload.get("strict_score"),
    78→    }
    79→
    80→    sample_items: list[dict] = []
    81→    items = payload.get("items")
    82→    if isinstance(items, list):
    83→        for item in items[:20]:
    84→            if not isinstance(item, dict):
    85→                continue
    86→            sample_items.append(
    87→                {
    88→                    "id": item.get("id"),
    89→                    "kind": item.get("kind"),
    90→                    "detector": item.get("detector"),
    91→                    "file": item.get("file"),
    92→                    "summary": _truncate_text(item.get("summary"), limit=200),
    93→                }
    94→            )
    95→    minimal["items"] = sample_items
    96→
    97→    if "config" in payload:
    98→        minimal["config"] = payload["config"]
    99→    if "config_error" in payload:
   100→        minimal["config_error"] = payload["config_error"]

    31→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    41→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    42→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    43→    plan = load_plan()
    44→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    45→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    46→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    47→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    48→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    49→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    50→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    51→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    52→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    53→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    54→
    55→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    56→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    57→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    58→
    59→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    60→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    61→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    62→    if total_skipped:
    63→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    64→    else:
    65→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    66→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    67→    if clusters:
    68→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    69→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    70→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    71→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    72→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    73→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    74→    if described or noted:

    76→    if active:
    77→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    78→    if superseded:
    79→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    80→
